diff --git a/app/api/dashboard/autopilot/route.ts b/app/api/dashboard/autopilot/route.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..41d4651
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/api/dashboard/autopilot/route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { runQualityAutopilot } from "@/lib/autopilot";
+import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
+import { jsonError, readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { guardPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+import { audit } from "@/lib/tenant";
+import { normalizeWebsite } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
+export const maxDuration = 120;
+
+const autopilotSchema = z.object({
+  targetUrl: z.string().trim().min(4).max(300),
+});
+
+/**
+ * POST /api/dashboard/autopilot { targetUrl }
+ * Runs the five Quality Autopilot phases: live crawl, Strix scan, Graphify,
+ * Bivia-calibrated authoring and immediate Studio execution.
+ */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("crawlRun", { write: true });
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const { ctx } = guard;
+
+  const parsed = autopilotSchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+
+  const targetUrl = normalizeWebsite(parsed.data.targetUrl);
+  if (!targetUrl) {
+    return jsonError("Enter a public URL, e.g. https://www.cgi.com", 400);
+  }
+
+  const result = await runQualityAutopilot(prisma, ctx, targetUrl);
+  await audit(
+    prisma,
+    ctx,
+    "autopilot.completed",
+    `${targetUrl} · ${result.testCases.length} test case(s) · ${result.security.grade} security grade · ${result.execution.passed}/${result.execution.total} passed`,
+  );
+
+  return NextResponse.json(result, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
+}
diff --git a/app/api/dashboard/bivia/ground/route.ts b/app/api/dashboard/bivia/ground/route.ts

index 0000000..d770f11
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/api/dashboard/bivia/ground/route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,94 @@
+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { biviaGround } from "@/lib/bivia";
+import { jsonError, readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { guardPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+import { readSettings } from "@/lib/tenant";
+import { normalizeWebsite } from "@/lib/utils";
+import { captureTargetScreenshot, inspectTargetDom } from "@/lib/web-executor";
+
+export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
+export const maxDuration = 60;
+
+const groundSchema = z.object({
+  targetUrl: z.string().trim().max(300).optional(),
+  targetDescription: z.string().trim().min(2).max(300),
+});
+
+/**
+ * POST /api/dashboard/bivia/ground
+ * Grounds a natural-language target on the rendered page. Playwright captures
+ * the screenshot + DOM when available; the deterministic Bivia fallback keeps
+ * the overlay usable offline.
+ */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("testCases");
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const { ctx } = guard;
+
+  const parsed = groundSchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+
+  const targetUrl =
+    normalizeWebsite(parsed.data.targetUrl ?? "") ??
+    normalizeWebsite(ctx.org.website ?? "") ??
+    "https://www.cgi.com";
+
+  let screenshotBase64: string | null = null;
+  let domSummary = "";
+  try {
+    const capture = await captureTargetScreenshot(targetUrl);
+    if (capture) {
+      screenshotBase64 = capture.screenshotBase64;
+      domSummary = capture.domSummary;
+    }
+  } catch {
+    screenshotBase64 = null;
+  }
+  if (!domSummary) {
+    try {
+      const snapshot = await inspectTargetDom(targetUrl);
+      if (snapshot) {
+        domSummary = JSON.stringify(snapshot).slice(0, 8_000);
+      }
+    } catch {
+      domSummary = "";
+    }
+  }
+
+  const analysisCode = readSettings(ctx.org).analysisCode ?? null;
+  const result = await biviaGround({
+    targetDescription: parsed.data.targetDescription,
+    screenshotBase64,
+    domSummary: domSummary || `Visual grounding for ${targetUrl}`,
+    organisationId: ctx.org.id,
+    sources: { analysisCode },
+    mode: "fast",
+  });
+  if (result.status === "abstained" && result.width <= 0 && result.height <= 0) {
+    return jsonError(result.abstainReason || "Bivia could not ground this target.", 422);
+  }
+
+  return NextResponse.json({
+    targetUrl,
+    targetDescription: parsed.data.targetDescription,
+    screenshotBase64,
+    screenshotCaptured: Boolean(screenshotBase64),
+    domCaptured: Boolean(domSummary),
+    result: {
+      x: result.x,
+      y: result.y,
+      width: result.width,
+      height: result.height,
+      rawBox: result.rawBox,
+      space: result.space,
+      label: result.label ?? parsed.data.targetDescription,
+      confidence: result.confidence,
+      confidencePct: Math.round(result.confidence * 1000) / 10,
+      status: result.status,
+      provider: result.provider,
+      latencyMs: result.latencyMs,
+      abstainReason: result.abstainReason,
+    },
+  });
+}
diff --git a/app/api/dashboard/pull-requests/gate/route.ts b/app/api/dashboard/pull-requests/gate/route.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9b506f1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/api/dashboard/pull-requests/gate/route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,151 @@
+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { biviaScoreAndSet } from "@/lib/bivia";
+import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
+import { jsonError, readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { parseIntegrationConfig } from "@/lib/integration-providers";
+import { buildPrGateMarkdown, type PrGateBivia, type PrGateImpact } from "@/lib/pr-gate";
+import { guardPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+import { runStrixScan } from "@/lib/strix";
+import { audit, readSettings } from "@/lib/tenant";
+import { normalizeWebsite } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
+export const maxDuration = 60;
+
+const gateSchema = z.object({ id: z.string().min(1).max(60) });
+
+function parseJson<T>(value: string | null | undefined, fallback: T): T {
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(value || "") as T;
+  } catch {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+}
+
+function asBivia(value: unknown): PrGateBivia | null {
+  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
+  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
+  const riskScore = Number(record.riskScore);
+  const probabilityPct = Number(record.probabilityPct);
+  const riskLevel = record.riskLevel;
+  if (!Number.isFinite(riskScore) || !Number.isFinite(probabilityPct)) return null;
+  if (riskLevel !== "low" && riskLevel !== "medium" && riskLevel !== "high") return null;
+  return {
+    riskScore,
+    probabilityPct,
+    riskLevel,
+    provider: typeof record.provider === "string" ? record.provider : "Articul8 Bivia",
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * POST /api/dashboard/pull-requests/gate { id }
+ * Builds the copy-ready CI/CD gate comment: Bivia verdict + calibrated risk,
+ * the `/set` impacted test table, and the Strix security summary.
+ */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("prRun");
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const { ctx } = guard;
+
+  const parsed = gateSchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+
+  const pr = await prisma.pullRequestCheck.findFirst({ where: { id: parsed.data.id, organisationId: ctx.org.id } });
+  if (!pr) return jsonError("Pull request not found.", 404);
+
+  const files = parseJson<string[]>(pr.changedFilesJson, []);
+  const impact = parseJson<PrGateImpact>(pr.impactJson, {});
+  let bivia = asBivia(impact.bivia);
+
+  if (!bivia) {
+    const candidateIds = (impact.impacted ?? []).map((test) => test.id).filter(Boolean);
+    const candidates = await prisma.testCase.findMany({
+      where: {
+        organisationId: ctx.org.id,
+        ...(candidateIds.length ? { id: { in: candidateIds } } : {}),
+      },
+      select: { id: true, title: true, module: true, criticality: true, status: true },
+      take: 12,
+    });
+    const analysisCode = readSettings(ctx.org).analysisCode ?? null;
+    let integrationConfig = null;
+    try {
+      const row = await prisma.integration.findUnique({
+        where: { organisationId_provider: { organisationId: ctx.org.id, provider: "BIVIA" } },
+        select: { configJson: true },
+      });
+      integrationConfig = row ? parseIntegrationConfig(row.configJson) : null;
+    } catch {
+      integrationConfig = null;
+    }
+    if (analysisCode?.biviaPrRisk !== false) {
+      const decision = await biviaScoreAndSet({
+        prTitle: pr.title,
+        changedFiles: files,
+        candidateTests: candidates,
+        organisationId: ctx.org.id,
+        sources: { integrationConfig, analysisCode },
+      });
+      bivia = {
+        riskScore: Math.round(decision.riskScore * 10) / 10,
+        riskLevel: decision.riskLevel,
+        probabilityPct: Math.round(decision.probability * 1000) / 10,
+        provider: decision.provider,
+      };
+      // Persist the calibrated gate inputs so the next preview is instant.
+      await prisma.pullRequestCheck.update({
+        where: { id: pr.id },
+        data: { impactJson: JSON.stringify({ ...impact, bivia }) },
+      });
+    }
+  }
+
+  const targetUrl =
+    normalizeWebsite(ctx.org.website ?? "") ??
+    normalizeWebsite(pr.externalUrl ?? "") ??
+    "https://www.cgi.com";
+  const strix = await runStrixScan(targetUrl);
+
+  const markdown = buildPrGateMarkdown({
+    organisation: ctx.org.name,
+    prNumber: pr.prNumber,
+    title: pr.title,
+    branch: pr.branch,
+    branchTarget: pr.branchTarget,
+    author: pr.author,
+    provider: pr.provider,
+    repository: pr.repository,
+    filesChanged: pr.filesChanged,
+    riskLevel: pr.riskLevel,
+    files,
+    impact,
+    bivia,
+    strix,
+    targetUrl,
+  });
+
+  await audit(
+    prisma,
+    ctx,
+    "pr.gate_generated",
+    `#${pr.prNumber} · Bivia ${bivia ? `${bivia.riskScore.toFixed(1)}/10 (${bivia.probabilityPct.toFixed(1)}%)` : "fallback"} · Strix ${strix.grade}`,
+  );
+
+  return NextResponse.json(
+    {
+      prNumber: pr.prNumber,
+      markdown,
+      bivia,
+      security: {
+        grade: strix.grade,
+        score: strix.score,
+        reachable: strix.reachable,
+        findings: strix.findings.filter((finding) => !finding.passed).length,
+      },
+      generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    },
+    { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
+  );
+}

+"use client";
+
+import Link from "next/link";
+import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
+import { useState } from "react";
+import {
+  CqaIcon,
+  ErrorNote,
+  Modal,
+  cqaBtnPrimary,
+  cqaBtnSecondary,
+  cqaInput,
+  cqaLabel,
+} from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import { apiFetch, errorMessage } from "@/lib/api";
+import type { AutopilotResult } from "@/lib/autopilot";
+import { toast } from "@/lib/toast";
+import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+const DEFAULT_PHASES: Array<{ id: AutopilotResult["phases"][number]["id"]; label: string; icon: string; hint: string }> = [
+  { id: "crawl", label: "Live DOM crawl", icon: "travel_explore", hint: "Capture the rendered page and map real flows." },
+  { id: "security", label: "Strix OWASP / header scan", icon: "shield", hint: "Passive headers, cookies, transport and OWASP surface." },
+  { id: "graph", label: "Graphify topology", icon: "account_tree", hint: "Project crawl, DOM and tests into one quality graph." },
+  { id: "authoring", label: "Context7 + Bivia authoring", icon: "auto_awesome", hint: "Grounded test cases with AI Verification and scored locators." },
+  { id: "execution", label: "Studio execution", icon: "play_circle", hint: "Run the new cases immediately in Test Studio." },
+];
+
+/**
+ * One-click Quality Autopilot launcher. The modal keeps all five phases
+ * visible while the server orchestrates them and links every artifact.
+ */
+export function AutopilotButton({
+  defaultTarget = "https://www.cgi.com",
+  readOnly = false,
+  className,
+  variant = "primary",
+}: {
+  defaultTarget?: string;
+  readOnly?: boolean;
+  className?: string;
+  variant?: "primary" | "secondary";
+}) {
+  const router = useRouter();
+  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
+  const [target, setTarget] = useState(defaultTarget);
+  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [result, setResult] = useState<AutopilotResult | null>(null);
+
+  async function launch() {
+    if (pending) return;
+    setPending(true);
+    setError(null);
+    setResult(null);
+    try {
+      const response = await apiFetch<AutopilotResult>("/api/dashboard/autopilot", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: { targetUrl: target.trim() },
+      });
+      setResult(response);
+      toast(
+        `🚀 Quality Autopilot complete · ${response.testCases.length} test case(s) · Strix ${response.security.grade} · ${response.execution.passed}/${response.execution.total} passed`,
+        "success",
+      );
+      router.refresh();
+    } catch (caught) {
+      setError(errorMessage(caught));
+    } finally {
+      setPending(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  const phaseById = new Map(result?.phases.map((phase) => [phase.id, phase]) ?? []);
+  const completed = result?.phases.filter((phase) => phase.status === "completed").length ?? 0;
+
+  return (
+    <>
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        data-testid="autopilot-open"
+        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
+        disabled={readOnly}
+        className={cn(variant === "primary" ? cqaBtnPrimary : cqaBtnSecondary, "h-9 px-3.5 text-[12.5px]", className)}
+      >
+        <CqaIcon name="rocket_launch" className="text-[17px]" /> 🚀 Launch Quality Autopilot
+      </button>
+
+      <Modal open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title="🚀 Quality Autopilot" size="xl">
+        <div className="space-y-4">
+          <p className="text-[12.5px] leading-5 text-[#6b7280]">
+            One click runs five live phases end to end: crawl the rendered app, scan its OWASP surface, build the Graphify
+            topology, author Bivia-calibrated test cases and execute them immediately in Test Studio.
+          </p>
+
+          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto]">
+            <label className="block">
+              <span className={cqaLabel}>Target URL</span>
+              <input
+                className={cqaInput}
+                value={target}
+                onChange={(event) => setTarget(event.target.value)}
+                placeholder="https://www.cgi.com"
+                data-testid="autopilot-target"
+              />
+            </label>
+            <div className="flex items-end gap-1.5">
+              {["https://gmailad.contextqa.com", "https://www.cgi.com"].map((url) => (
+                <button
+                  key={url}
+                  type="button"
+                  onClick={() => setTarget(url)}
+                  className="h-[38px] rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2.5 text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#4b5563] transition hover:border-[#c7d2fe] hover:bg-[#f5f3ff]"
+                >
+                  {url.replace("https://", "")}
+                </button>
+              ))}
+            </div>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              data-testid="autopilot-run"
+              onClick={() => void launch()}
+              disabled={readOnly || pending || !target.trim()}
+              className={cn(cqaBtnPrimary, "h-9 px-4")}
+            >
+              <CqaIcon name={pending ? "hourglass_top" : "rocket_launch"} className="text-[17px]" />
+              {pending ? "Autopilot running…" : result ? "Run again" : "🚀 Launch Quality Autopilot"}
+            </button>
+            {result && (
+              <span className="rounded-full bg-[#ecfdf3] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#067647]">
+                {completed}/5 phases completed
+              </span>
+            )}
+            {result && (
+              <Link href={result.links.graphify} className="text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-[#5b4ee8] hover:underline">
+                Open Graphify map
+              </Link>
+            )}
+          </div>
+
+          <ErrorNote error={error} />
+
+          <ol className="space-y-2" data-testid="autopilot-phases">
+            {DEFAULT_PHASES.map((definition, index) => {
+              const phase = phaseById.get(definition.id);
+              const running = pending && !result && index === 0;
+              return (
+                <li
+                  key={definition.id}
+                  data-testid={`autopilot-phase-${definition.id}`}
+                  className={cn(
+                    "flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2.5 transition",
+                    phase?.status === "failed"
+                      ? "border-[#fecdca] bg-[#fef3f2]"
+                      : phase?.status === "completed"
+                        ? "border-[#abefc6] bg-[#f0fdf4]"
+                        : running
+                          ? "border-[#c7d2fe] bg-[#eef2ff]"
+                          : "border-[#eef0f3] bg-[#fcfcfd]",
+                  )}
+                >
+                  <span
+                    className={cn(
+                      "mt-0.5 flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold",
+                      phase?.status === "failed"
+                        ? "bg-[#fee4e2] text-[#b42318]"
+                        : phase?.status === "completed"
+                          ? "bg-[#dcfae6] text-[#067647]"
+                          : running
+                            ? "animate-pulse bg-[#e0e7ff] text-[#4f46e5]"
+                            : "bg-[#f1f5f9] text-[#64748b]",
+                    )}
+                  >
+                    {phase?.status === "completed" ? "✓" : phase?.status === "failed" ? "!" : index + 1}
+                  </span>
+                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
+                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">
+                        <CqaIcon name={definition.icon} className="text-[14px] text-[#5b4ee8]" /> {definition.label}
+                      </span>
+                      <span className="text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#9ca3af]">
+                        {pending && !result ? (running ? "running…" : "queued") : phase ? phase.metric : "waiting"}
+                      </span>
+                      {phase?.href && (
+                        <Link href={phase.href} className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-[#5b4ee8] hover:underline">
+                          Open
+                        </Link>
+                      )}
+                    </div>
+                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] leading-4 text-[#6b7280]">{phase?.detail ?? definition.hint}</p>
+                  </div>
+                </li>
+              );
+            })}
+          </ol>
+
+          {result && (
+            <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-[#fafafa] p-3" data-testid="autopilot-results">
+              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+                <span className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">Autopilot artifacts</span>
+                <span className="rounded-full bg-white px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#4b5563]">
+                  Strix {result.security.grade} · {result.security.score}/100
+                </span>
+                <span className="rounded-full bg-white px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#4b5563]">
+                  {result.graph.nodes} nodes · {result.graph.edges} edges
+                </span>
+                <span className="rounded-full bg-white px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#4b5563]">
+                  {result.execution.passed}/{result.execution.total} passed
+                </span>
+              </div>
+              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
+                {result.testCases.map((testCase) => (
+                  <Link
+                    key={testCase.id}
+                    href={testCase.href}
+                    data-testid="autopilot-test-link"
+                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-[#c7d2fe] bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#4f46e5] hover:bg-[#eef2ff]"
+                  >
+                    <CqaIcon name="fact_check" className="text-[13px]" /> {testCase.title}
+                  </Link>
+                ))}
+                <Link
+                  href={result.links.security}
+                  data-testid="autopilot-strix-link"
+                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#374151] hover:bg-[#f9fafb]"
+                >
+                  <CqaIcon name="shield" className="text-[13px]" /> Open Strix report
+                </Link>
+                <Link
+                  href={result.links.graphify}
+                  data-testid="autopilot-graph-link"
+                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#374151] hover:bg-[#f9fafb]"
+                >
+                  <CqaIcon name="account_tree" className="text-[13px]" /> Open Graphify map
+                </Link>
+                <Link
+                  href={result.links.crawl}
+                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#374151] hover:bg-[#f9fafb]"
+                >
+                  <CqaIcon name="travel_explore" className="text-[13px]" /> Open crawl
+                </Link>
+              </div>
+              <p className="mt-2 text-[10.5px] text-[#9ca3af]">
+                Crawl {result.crawlId} · completed {new Date(result.completedAt).toLocaleTimeString("en-US")}
+              </p>
+            </div>
+          )}
+        </div>
+      </Modal>
+    </>
+  );
+}

+"use client";
+
+import { useState } from "react";
+import { CqaIcon } from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+export interface GroundingBoxView {
+  x: number;
+  y: number;
+  width: number;
+  height: number;
+  rawBox?: [number, number, number, number];
+  space?: string;
+  label?: string;
+  confidence?: number;
+  provider?: string;
+  status?: string;
+  abstainReason?: string;
+}
+
+function confidencePct(value: number | undefined) {
+  const raw = Number(value ?? 0);
+  if (!Number.isFinite(raw) || raw <= 0) return 0;
+  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, raw <= 1 ? raw * 100 : raw));
+}
+
+function VisualPreview() {
+  return (
+    <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#f8fafc] via-white to-[#eef2ff]">
+      <div className="flex h-7 items-center gap-1 border-b border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2">
+        <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#fca5a5]" />
+        <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#fcd34d]" />
+        <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#86efac]" />
+        <span className="ml-2 h-3 flex-1 rounded bg-[#f1f5f9]" />
+      </div>
+      <div className="grid h-[calc(100%-28px)] grid-cols-[24%_1fr]">
+        <div className="space-y-2 border-r border-[#eef0f3] bg-white/70 p-2.5">
+          <div className="h-2 w-14 rounded bg-[#cbd5e1]" />
+          <div className="h-1.5 w-full rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+          <div className="h-1.5 w-4/5 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+          <div className="h-1.5 w-3/5 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+          <div className="mt-3 h-6 rounded-md bg-[#eef2ff]" />
+          <div className="h-1.5 w-5/6 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+          <div className="h-1.5 w-2/3 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+        </div>
+        <div className="space-y-2 p-3">
+          <div className="h-2.5 w-32 rounded bg-[#94a3b8]" />
+          <div className="h-1.5 w-2/3 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+          <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
+            {[0, 1, 2].map((index) => (
+              <div key={index} className="space-y-1.5 rounded-md border border-[#eef0f3] bg-white p-2">
+                <div className="h-4 w-4 rounded bg-[#e0e7ff]" />
+                <div className="h-1.5 w-4/5 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+                <div className="h-1.5 w-3/5 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+              </div>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+          <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 w-full rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+          <div className="h-1.5 w-5/6 rounded bg-[#e2e8f0]" />
+        </div>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Neon visual-grounding overlay. Coordinates arrive normalized on a 0–1000
+ * canvas, so each value is divided by 10 to obtain a percentage. The badge
+ * always exposes the raw `[x1, y1, x2, y2]` box from Bivia.
+ */
+export function BiviaGroundingCanvas({
+  box,
+  confidence,
+  label,
+  imageSrc,
+  title = "Visual preview",
+  className,
+  compact = false,
+}: {
+  box: GroundingBoxView;
+  confidence?: number;
+  label?: string;
+  imageSrc?: string | null;
+  title?: string;
+  className?: string;
+  compact?: boolean;
+}) {
+  const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);
+  const pct = confidencePct(confidence ?? box.confidence);
+  const rawBox: [number, number, number, number] =
+    Array.isArray(box.rawBox) && box.rawBox.length === 4
+      ? box.rawBox
+      : [Math.round(box.x), Math.round(box.y), Math.round(box.x + box.width), Math.round(box.y + box.height)];
+  const left = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, box.x / 10));
+  const top = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, box.y / 10));
+  const width = Math.max(1.5, Math.min(100 - left, box.width / 10));
+  const height = Math.max(1.5, Math.min(100 - top, box.height / 10));
+  const caption = label || box.label || "Bivia grounded target";
+
+  return (
+    <figure className={cn("min-w-0", className)} data-testid="bivia-grounding-canvas">
+      <div className="relative aspect-[16/9] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[#dbe2ea] bg-white shadow-inner">
+        {imageSrc ? (
+          // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
+          <img src={imageSrc} alt={`${title} screenshot`} className="absolute inset-0 size-full object-cover" />
+        ) : (
+          <VisualPreview />
+        )}
+        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[#0f172a]/5" />
+        <div
+          data-testid="bivia-grounding-overlay"
+          role="img"
+          tabIndex={0}
+          aria-label={`Bivia grounded ${caption} at ${rawBox.join(", ")} with ${pct.toFixed(1)}% confidence`}
+          onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(true)}
+          onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(false)}
+          onFocus={() => setHovered(true)}
+          onBlur={() => setHovered(false)}
+          className={cn(
+            "absolute cursor-crosshair rounded-[5px] border-2 border-[#22d3ee] bg-[#22d3ee]/15 outline-none transition duration-150",
+            "shadow-[0_0_14px_rgba(34,211,238,0.65),inset_0_0_10px_rgba(34,211,238,0.18)]",
+            hovered && "border-[#67e8f9] bg-[#67e8f9]/25 shadow-[0_0_24px_rgba(103,232,249,0.9)]",
+          )}
+          style={{ left: `${left}%`, top: `${top}%`, width: `${width}%`, height: `${height}%` }}
+        >
+          <span className="absolute -left-[3px] -top-[3px] size-2 rounded-full border border-white bg-[#22d3ee] shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(34,211,238,0.9)]" />
+          <span className="absolute -right-[3px] -bottom-[3px] size-2 rounded-full border border-white bg-[#22d3ee] shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(34,211,238,0.9)]" />
+          {hovered && (

+              {caption}
+            </span>
+          )}
+        </div>
+        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-2 bottom-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
+          <span

index 0000000..f63cb6c

+++ b/components/dashboard/pages/BiviaGroundingLab.tsx

+"use client";
+
+import { useState } from "react";
+import { BiviaGroundingCanvas, type GroundingBoxView } from "@/components/dashboard/pages/BiviaGroundingCanvas";
+import { CqaIcon, ErrorNote, cqaBtnPrimary, cqaCard, cqaInput, cqaLabel } from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import { apiFetch, errorMessage } from "@/lib/api";
+import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+interface GroundResponse {
+  targetUrl: string;
+  screenshotBase64: string | null;
+  screenshotCaptured: boolean;
+  domCaptured: boolean;
+  result: GroundingBoxView & { confidencePct: number; latencyMs: number };
+}
+
+/** Interactive Bivia visual-grounding lab for /dashboard/visual-testing. */
+export function BiviaGroundingLab({
+  defaultTarget,
+  initialBox,
+}: {
+  defaultTarget: string;
+  initialBox: GroundingBoxView;
+}) {
+  const [targetUrl, setTargetUrl] = useState(defaultTarget);
+  const [description, setDescription] = useState("Primary call-to-action button");
+  const [box, setBox] = useState<GroundingBoxView>(initialBox);
+  const [confidence, setConfidence] = useState(initialBox.confidence ?? 0.88);
+  const [image, setImage] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [meta, setMeta] = useState<string>("");
+
+  async function ground() {
+    setBusy(true);
+    setError(null);
+    try {
+      const response = await apiFetch<GroundResponse>("/api/dashboard/bivia/ground", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: { targetUrl, targetDescription: description.trim() || "Primary call-to-action button" },
+      });
+      setBox(response.result);
+      setConfidence(response.result.confidence ?? 0);
+      setImage(response.screenshotBase64 ? `data:image/png;base64,${response.screenshotBase64}` : null);
+      setMeta(
+        `${response.result.provider} · ${response.result.latencyMs} ms · ${response.domCaptured ? "DOM captured" : "synthetic DOM"} · ${
+          response.screenshotCaptured ? "screenshot captured" : "visual preview"
+        }`,
+      );
+    } catch (caught) {
+      setError(errorMessage(caught));
+    } finally {
+      setBusy(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <section className={cn(cqaCard, "overflow-hidden")} data-testid="bivia-grounding-lab">
+      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[#f0f1f3] px-4 py-3">
+        <div>
+          <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">
+            <span className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#ecfeff] text-[15px]">🎯</span>
+            Bivia visual grounding canvas
+          </h2>
+          <p className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] leading-4 text-[#6b7280]">
+            Ground any UI target to a normalized bounding box with a neon overlay. Coordinates are shown exactly as Bivia returns them.
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[#ecfeff] px-2.5 py-1 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-[#0e7490]">
+          <CqaIcon name="my_location" className="text-[13px]" /> 0–1000 canvas
+        </span>
+      </header>
+      <div className="grid gap-4 px-4 py-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_300px]">
+        <BiviaGroundingCanvas box={box} confidence={confidence} imageSrc={image} title="Bivia grounding preview" />
+        <form
+          className="space-y-3"
+          onSubmit={(event) => {
+            event.preventDefault();
+            void ground();
+          }}
+        >
+          <label className="block">
+            <span className={cqaLabel}>Target URL</span>
+            <input className={cqaInput} value={targetUrl} onChange={(event) => setTargetUrl(event.target.value)} placeholder="https://www.cgi.com" />
+          </label>
+          <label className="block">
+            <span className={cqaLabel}>Target description</span>
+            <input
+              className={cqaInput}
+              value={description}
+              onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
+              placeholder="Primary call-to-action button"
+            />
+          </label>
+          <button type="submit" data-testid="bivia-ground-run" disabled={busy} className={cn(cqaBtnPrimary, "w-full")}>
+            <CqaIcon name={busy ? "hourglass_top" : "my_location"} className="text-[16px]" />
+            {busy ? "Grounding…" : "🎯 Run Bivia Visual Grounding"}
+          </button>
+          <ErrorNote error={error} />
+          <p className="text-[10.5px] leading-4 text-[#9ca3af]">
+            Playwright captures the rendered viewport when available. Offline runs use the calibrated deterministic box.
+          </p>
+          {meta && <p data-testid="bivia-ground-meta" className="rounded-md bg-[#f8fafc] px-2.5 py-2 text-[10.5px] leading-4 text-[#475569]">{meta}</p>}
+        </form>
+      </div>
+    </section>
+  );
+}

+  value,
+  hint,
+  testId,
+  icon,
+}: {
+  label: string;
+  value: string;
+  hint?: string;

+        {value}
+      </p>

+  );

+            </p>
+          </div>
+          <div className="text-right">

+          </div>

+        title="📋 CI/CD PR Gate Comment"
+        size="xl"
+        footer={
+          <>
+            <button type="button" className={cqaBtnSecondary} onClick={() => setGateOpen(false)}>
+              Close
+            </button>
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              className={cqaBtnPrimary}
+              data-testid="pr-gate-copy"
+              disabled={!gateMarkdown}
+              onClick={() => void copyGate()}
+            >
+              <CqaIcon name="content_copy" className="text-[16px]" /> Copy Markdown Report
+            </button>
+          </>
+        }
+      >
+        <div className="space-y-3">
+          <p className="text-[12.5px] leading-5 text-[#6b7280]">
+            Copy-ready merge gate for GitHub, GitLab, Azure DevOps or Jenkins. It combines the calibrated Bivia verdict,
+            the <code className="rounded bg-[#f3f4f6] px-1 py-0.5 text-[11px]">/set</code> impacted-test table and the Strix security summary.
+          </p>
+          {gateError && (
+            <div role="alert" className="rounded-lg border border-[#fecdca] bg-[#fef3f2] px-3 py-2 text-[12px] text-[#b42318]">
+              {gateError}
+            </div>
+          )}
+          {gatePending && (
+            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#c7d2fe] bg-[#eef2ff] px-3 py-2 text-[12px] text-[#4f46e5]">
+              <CqaIcon name="hourglass_top" className="animate-pulse text-[16px]" /> Calibrating Bivia risk and running Strix…
+            </div>
+          )}
+          {gateMeta && (
+            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
+              <span className="rounded-full bg-[#f5f3ff] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-[#5b4ee8]">
+                🧠 Bivia {gateMeta.bivia ? `${gateMeta.bivia.riskScore.toFixed(1)}/10 · ${gateMeta.bivia.probabilityPct.toFixed(1)}%` : "fallback"}
+              </span>
+              <span className="rounded-full bg-[#ecfdf3] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-[#067647]">
+                🛡️ Strix {gateMeta.security?.grade ?? "?"} · {gateMeta.security?.score ?? 0}/100 · {gateMeta.security?.findings ?? 0} finding(s)
+              </span>
+            </div>
+          )}
+          {gateMarkdown ? (
+            <pre
+              data-testid="pr-gate-markdown"
+              className="max-h-[55vh] overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-lg border border-[#eef0f3] bg-[#0f172a] p-3 font-mono text-[11px] leading-5 text-[#e2e8f0]"
+            >
+              {gateMarkdown}
+            </pre>
+          ) : (
+            !gatePending &&
+            !gateError && <p className="text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af]">No gate report generated yet.</p>
+          )}
+        </div>
+      </Modal>
     </div>
   );
 }

+  async function simulateSelfHeal() {
+    if (selfHealing) return;
+    setSelfHealing(true);
+    try {
+      const result = await apiFetch<SelfHealResponse>(`/api/dashboard/test-cases/${data.id}`, {
+        method: "PATCH",
+        json: { action: "simulate_self_heal", stepId: detailStep?.id },
+      });
+      if (Array.isArray(result.steps)) setSteps(result.steps);
+      toast(
+        `✨ Self-healed by Bivia · ${result.oldSelector} → ${result.newSelector} · ${result.probabilityPct.toFixed(1)}% calibrated`,
+        "success",
+      );
+      router.refresh();
+    } catch (caught) {
+      toast(errorMessage(caught), "error");
+    } finally {
+      setSelfHealing(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function runGrounding() {
+    if (!detailStep || groundBusy) return;
+    setGroundBusy(true);
+    setGroundError(null);
+    try {
+      const response = await apiFetch<{
+        screenshotBase64: string | null;
+        screenshotCaptured: boolean;
+        domCaptured: boolean;
+        result: GroundingBoxView & { confidencePct: number; latencyMs: number };
+      }>("/api/dashboard/bivia/ground", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: {
+          targetDescription: detailStep.target?.trim() || `${detailStep.action} target`,
+        },
+      });
+      setGroundBox(response.result);
+      setGroundImage(response.screenshotBase64 ? `data:image/png;base64,${response.screenshotBase64}` : null);
+      setGroundMeta(
+        `${response.result.provider} · ${response.result.latencyMs} ms · ${response.domCaptured ? "DOM captured" : "synthetic DOM"} · ${
+          response.screenshotCaptured ? "screenshot captured" : "visual preview"
+        }`,
+      );
+    } catch (caught) {
+      setGroundError(errorMessage(caught));
+    } finally {
+      setGroundBusy(false);
+    }
+  }

diff --git a/lib/autopilot.ts b/lib/autopilot.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..4c2e095

+++ b/lib/autopilot.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,455 @@
+/**
+ * Quality Autopilot — one-click orchestration of the autonomous quality loop.
+ *
+ *   1. Live DOM crawl (`inspectTargetDom`, crawl session, mapped flows)
+ *   2. Strix passive OWASP/header scan (persisted SecurityScan)
+ *   3. Graphify topology (crawl → DOM → test → PR graph)
+ *   4. Context7 + Bivia-calibrated test authoring (AI Verification + scored locators)
+ *   5. Immediate Studio execution (real TestRun rows)
+ *
+ * Every phase is persisted so the UI can link to the exact crawl, scan,
+ * Graphify map and Test Studio execution that the autopilot produced.
+ */
+import type { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
+import type { BiviaConfigSources } from "./bivia";
+import { context7Guidance } from "./context7";
+import { runTestCases, startCrawl } from "./engine";
+import { loadGraphifyMap } from "./graphify";
+import { parseIntegrationConfig } from "./integration-providers";
+import { generateCalibratedMagicLocators } from "./magic-locator";
+import { runStrixScan, type StrixReport } from "./strix";
+import { readSettings, type OrgContext } from "./tenant";
+import {
+  groundedFlowsFromDom,
+  groundedStepsForFlow,
+  inspectTargetDom,
+  type GroundedFlow,
+  type TargetDomSnapshot,
+} from "./web-executor";
+import { normalizeWebsite } from "./utils";
+
+type Actor = Pick<OrgContext, "org" | "user">;
+
+export type AutopilotPhaseId = "crawl" | "security" | "graph" | "authoring" | "execution";
+
+export interface AutopilotPhase {
+  id: AutopilotPhaseId;
+  label: string;
+  status: "completed" | "failed";
+  detail: string;
+  metric: string;
+  href?: string;
+}
+
+export interface AutopilotTestCaseResult {
+  id: string;
+  title: string;
+  module: string;
+  steps: number;
+  verifySteps: number;
+  calibratedLocators: number;
+  confidencePct: number;
+  status: string;
+  href: string;
+}
+
+export interface AutopilotResult {
+  targetUrl: string;
+  startedAt: string;
+  completedAt: string;
+  crawlId: string;
+  phases: AutopilotPhase[];
+  security: {
+    scanId: string;
+    grade: StrixReport["grade"];
+    score: number;
+    reachable: boolean;
+    high: number;
+    medium: number;
+    low: number;
+    findings: number;
+    href: string;
+  };
+  graph: { nodes: number; edges: number; href: string };
+  testCases: AutopilotTestCaseResult[];
+  execution: { passed: number; failed: number; total: number; autoFix: boolean };
+  links: { crawl: string; security: string; graphify: string; studio: string | null };
+}
+
+function hostOf(targetUrl: string): string {
+  try {
+    return new URL(targetUrl).host;
+  } catch {
+    return targetUrl;
+  }
+}
+
+function moduleOf(flow: GroundedFlow): string {
+  return flow.module || "Core";
+}
+
+/** Deterministic synthetic steps used only when the live DOM is unreachable. */
+function fallbackFlows(targetUrl: string): GroundedFlow[] {
+  const host = hostOf(targetUrl);
+  return [
+    {
+      name: `Verify ${host} landing page · Autopilot`,
+      module: "Core",
+      steps: 4,
+      criticality: "high",
+      description: `Fallback autopilot flow for ${targetUrl}.`,
+    },
+    {
+      name: `Check ${host} primary navigation · Autopilot`,
+      module: "Core",
+      steps: 3,
+      criticality: "medium",
+      description: `Fallback navigation flow for ${targetUrl}.`,
+    },
+  ];
+}
+
+/**
+ * Runs the full autopilot. Network phases are best-effort: an unreachable
+ * target still produces deterministic flows, a Strix report and a runnable
+ * Studio execution so the operator always gets a useful result.
+ */
+export async function runQualityAutopilot(
+  db: PrismaClient,
+  actor: Actor,
+  targetInput: string,
+): Promise<AutopilotResult> {
+  const startedAt = new Date();
+  const targetUrl =
+    normalizeWebsite(targetInput) ??
+    normalizeWebsite(actor.org.website ?? "") ??
+    "https://www.cgi.com";
+
+  const settings = readSettings(actor.org);
+  const analysisCode = settings.analysisCode ?? null;
+  let integrationConfig = null;
+  try {
+    const row = await db.integration.findUnique({
+      where: { organisationId_provider: { organisationId: actor.org.id, provider: "BIVIA" } },
+      select: { configJson: true },
+    });
+    integrationConfig = row ? parseIntegrationConfig(row.configJson) : null;
+  } catch {
+    integrationConfig = null;
+  }
+  const biviaSources: BiviaConfigSources = { integrationConfig, analysisCode };
+
+  const phases: AutopilotPhase[] = [];
+  const linkCrawl = "/dashboard/crawl";
+  const linkSecurity = "/dashboard/security-testing";
+  const linkGraphify = "/dashboard/code-analysis";
+
+  // ── Phase 1 · Live DOM crawl ────────────────────────────────────────────
+  let snapshot: TargetDomSnapshot | null = null;
+  try {
+    snapshot = await inspectTargetDom(targetUrl);
+  } catch {
+    snapshot = null;
+  }
+  let flows: GroundedFlow[] = groundedFlowsFromDom(snapshot);
+  if (flows.length === 0) flows = fallbackFlows(targetUrl);
+  flows = flows.slice(0, 6);
+
+  // A running crawl short-circuits startCrawl; retire it first so every
+  // autopilot run owns a fresh, linked crawl session.
+  try {
+    await db.crawlSession.updateMany({
+      where: { organisationId: actor.org.id, status: "running" },
+      data: { status: "completed", completedAt: new Date() },
+    });
+  } catch {
+    /* no running crawl */
+  }
+  const crawl = await startCrawl(db, actor, targetUrl, { publishMode: "Require approval" });
+  const pagesVisited = Math.max(1, snapshot ? 1 + snapshot.navLinks.length + snapshot.forms.length : flows.length);
+  const crawlLogs = [
+    { offsetMs: 0, tag: "INFO", message: `Quality Autopilot started · ${targetUrl}` },
+    {
+      offsetMs: 400,
+      tag: "MAP",
+      message: snapshot
+        ? `Live DOM captured · "${snapshot.title || hostOf(targetUrl)}" · ${snapshot.navLinks.length} links · ${snapshot.buttons.length} buttons · ${snapshot.forms.length} forms`
+        : `Live DOM unavailable · deterministic crawl plan for ${hostOf(targetUrl)}`,
+    },
+    { offsetMs: 900, tag: "MAP", message: `${flows.length} flows mapped across ${pagesVisited} pages` },
+    { offsetMs: 1_400, tag: "DONE", message: `Autopilot crawl complete · ${flows.length} flows ready for authoring` },
+  ];
+  await db.crawlSession.update({
+    where: { id: crawl.id },
+    data: {
+      status: "completed",
+      completedAt: new Date(),
+      pagesVisited,
+      flowsMapped: flows.length,
+      logs: JSON.stringify(crawlLogs),
+    },
+  });
+  phases.push({
+    id: "crawl",
+    label: "Live DOM crawl",
+    status: snapshot ? "completed" : "failed",
+    detail: snapshot
+      ? `Captured "${snapshot.title || hostOf(targetUrl)}" with ${snapshot.navLinks.length} navigation links and ${snapshot.forms.length} forms.`
+      : "Target was unreachable; the deterministic crawl plan keeps the pipeline moving.",
+    metric: `${pagesVisited} pages · ${flows.length} flows`,
+    href: `${linkCrawl}?session=${crawl.id}`,
+  });
+
+  // ── Phase 2 · Strix OWASP/header scan ──────────────────────────────────
+  let report: StrixReport;
+  try {
+    report = await runStrixScan(targetUrl);
+  } catch {
+    report = {
+      target: targetUrl,
+      scannedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+      reachable: false,
+      httpStatus: null,
+      score: 0,
+      grade: "F",
+      findings: [],
+      headers: {},
+      cookies: [],
+      surface: [],
+      notes: [],
+      error: "Strix scan unavailable",
+    };
+  }
+  const failedFindings = report.findings.filter((finding) => !finding.passed);
+  const high = failedFindings.filter((finding) => finding.severity === "critical" || finding.severity === "high").length;
+  const medium = failedFindings.filter((finding) => finding.severity === "medium").length;
+  const low = failedFindings.filter((finding) => finding.severity === "low" || finding.severity === "info").length;
+  let scanId = "";
+  try {
+    const scan = await db.securityScan.create({
+      data: {
+        organisationId: actor.org.id,
+        targetUrl,
+        scanType: "Strix OWASP / headers",
+        status: "completed",
+        highCount: high,
+        mediumCount: medium,
+        lowCount: low,
+        findingsJson: JSON.stringify({
+          grade: report.grade,
+          score: report.score,
+          reachable: report.reachable,
+          httpStatus: report.httpStatus,
+          findings: report.findings,
+          headers: report.headers,
+        }),
+        completedAt: new Date(),
+      },
+    });
+    scanId = scan.id;
+  } catch {
+    scanId = "";
+  }
+  phases.push({
+    id: "security",
+    label: "Strix OWASP / header scan",
+    status: report.reachable ? "completed" : "failed",
+    detail: report.reachable
+      ? `Grade ${report.grade} (${report.score}/100) · ${failedFindings.length} finding(s) across OWASP and transport headers.`
+      : "Strix could not reach the target; the scan is recorded for audit.",
+    metric: `${high} high · ${medium} medium · ${low} low`,
+    href: linkSecurity,
+  });
+
+  // ── Phase 3 · Graphify topology ────────────────────────────────────────
+  let graph = { nodes: 0, edges: 0 };
+  try {
+    const map = await loadGraphifyMap(actor.org.id, { crawlId: crawl.id });
+    graph = { nodes: map.nodes.length, edges: map.edges.length };
+  } catch {
+    graph = { nodes: 0, edges: 0 };
+  }
+  phases.push({
+    id: "graph",
+    label: "Graphify topology",
+    status: "completed",
+    detail: "Crawl URLs, discovered DOM components, test cases and pull requests projected into one connected quality graph.",
+    metric: `${graph.nodes} nodes · ${graph.edges} edges`,
+    href: linkGraphify,
+  });
+
+  // ── Phase 4 · Context7 + Bivia-calibrated authoring ────────────────────
+  const guidance = context7Guidance("playwright accessible locator test case generation", 3);
+  const authoringFlows = flows.slice(0, 2);
+  const created: AutopilotTestCaseResult[] = [];
+  let calibratedLocators = 0;
+  let calibrationTotal = 0;
+  let calibrationConfidence = 0;
+
+  for (const flow of authoringFlows) {
+    const title = `${flow.name} — Autopilot`;
+    const count = Math.max(4, Math.min(7, flow.steps));
+    const steps: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = groundedStepsForFlow(snapshot, targetUrl, flow.name, count + 1).map((step) => ({ ...step }));
+    if (!steps.some((step) => step.action === "AI Assertion")) {
+      steps.push({
+        id: `s${steps.length + 1}`,
+        action: "AI Assertion",
+        target: `Verify "${flow.name}" completes with no console or network errors`,
+        value: "",
+        selector: "",
+      });
+    }
+    for (const step of steps) {
+      if (step.action === "AI Assertion") {
+        step.context7 = guidance.slice(0, 900);
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (calibratedLocators >= 2) continue;
+      if (!["Click", "Enter Text", "Verify Element"].includes(String(step.action))) continue;
+      try {
+        const calibration = await generateCalibratedMagicLocators(
+          {
+            action: String(step.action),
+            target: String(step.target),
+            value: String(step.value ?? ""),
+            selector: String(step.selector ?? ""),
+          },
+          { organisationId: actor.org.id, sources: biviaSources, enabled: true },
+        );
+        const best = calibration.best;
+        if (best) {
+          step.selector = best.selector;
+          step.locatorStrategy = best.strategy;
+          step.locatorScore = best.score;
+          if (calibration.bivia) {
+            step.calibratedProbability = calibration.bivia.probabilityPct;
+            step.biviaProvider = calibration.bivia.provider;
+            calibratedLocators += 1;
+            calibrationTotal += 1;
+            calibrationConfidence += calibration.bivia.probabilityPct;
+          }
+        }
+      } catch {
+        /* keep the deterministic selector */
+      }
+    }
+
+    const variables = { url: targetUrl };
+    const description = `Generated by Quality Autopilot from ${targetUrl}. ${flow.description}`;
+    const existing = await db.testCase.findFirst({
+      where: { organisationId: actor.org.id, title },
+      select: { id: true },
+    });
+    const testCase = existing
+      ? await db.testCase.update({
+          where: { id: existing.id },
+          data: {
+            module: moduleOf(flow),
+            description,
+            criticality: flow.criticality,
+            status: "draft",
+            stepsCount: Math.max(1, steps.length),
+            stepsJson: JSON.stringify(steps),
+            variablesJson: JSON.stringify(variables),
+            source: "ai",
+          },
+        })
+      : await db.testCase.create({
+          data: {
+            organisationId: actor.org.id,
+            title,
+            module: moduleOf(flow),
+            description,
+            criticality: flow.criticality,
+            status: "draft",
+            stepsCount: Math.max(1, steps.length),
+            stepsJson: JSON.stringify(steps),
+            variablesJson: JSON.stringify(variables),
+            source: "ai",
+          },
+        });
+    created.push({
+      id: testCase.id,
+      title: testCase.title,
+      module: testCase.module,
+      steps: steps.length,
+      verifySteps: steps.filter((step) => step.action === "AI Assertion").length,
+      calibratedLocators: steps.filter((step) => typeof step.calibratedProbability === "number").length,
+      confidencePct: calibrationTotal > 0 ? Math.round((calibrationConfidence / calibrationTotal) * 10) / 10 : 0,
+      status: testCase.status,
+      href: `/dashboard/test-studio/${testCase.id}`,
+    });
+  }
+
+  const aiVerifications = created.reduce((sum, testCase) => sum + testCase.verifySteps, 0);
+  const avgConfidence = calibrationTotal > 0 ? Math.round((calibrationConfidence / calibrationTotal) * 10) / 10 : 0;
+  phases.push({
+    id: "authoring",
+    label: "Context7 + Bivia test authoring",
+    status: created.length > 0 ? "completed" : "failed",
+    detail: `${created.length} grounded test case(s) authored with AI Verification steps, Context7 Playwright guidance and Bivia-calibrated locators.`,
+    metric: `${aiVerifications} AI verification · ${calibratedLocators} calibrated locator(s) · ${avgConfidence.toFixed(1)}% confidence`,
+    href: created[0]?.href,
+  });
+
+  // ── Phase 5 · Immediate Studio execution ───────────────────────────────
+  let execution = { passed: 0, failed: 0, total: 0, autoFix: false };
+  if (created.length > 0) {
+    const cases = await db.testCase.findMany({ where: { organisationId: actor.org.id, id: { in: created.map((testCase) => testCase.id) } } });
+    try {
+      const result = await runTestCases(db, actor, cases);
+      execution = { passed: result.passed, failed: result.failed, total: result.total, autoFix: false };
+    } catch {
+      execution = { passed: 0, failed: created.length, total: created.length, autoFix: false };
+    }
+    const refreshed = await db.testCase.findMany({
+      where: { organisationId: actor.org.id, id: { in: created.map((testCase) => testCase.id) } },
+      select: { id: true, status: true, stepsJson: true },
+    });
+    const statusById = new Map(refreshed.map((testCase) => [testCase.id, testCase.status]));
+    for (const testCase of created) {
+      testCase.status = statusById.get(testCase.id) ?? testCase.status;
+    }
+    execution.autoFix = refreshed.some((testCase) => testCase.stepsJson.includes("biviaHeal"));
+  }
+  phases.push({
+    id: "execution",
+    label: "Studio execution",
+    status: execution.failed === 0 && execution.total > 0 ? "completed" : "failed",
+    detail:
+      execution.total > 0
+        ? `${execution.passed}/${execution.total} test case(s) passed in Test Studio immediately after authoring.`
+        : "No authored case was available to execute.",
+    metric: `${execution.passed} passed · ${execution.failed} failed`,
+    href: created[0]?.href,
+  });
+
+  const completedAt = new Date();
+  return {
+    targetUrl,
+    startedAt: startedAt.toISOString(),
+    completedAt: completedAt.toISOString(),
+    crawlId: crawl.id,
+    phases,
+    security: {
+      scanId,
+      grade: report.grade,
+      score: report.score,
+      reachable: report.reachable,
+      high,
+      medium,
+      low,
+      findings: failedFindings.length,
+      href: linkSecurity,
+    },
+    graph: { ...graph, href: linkGraphify },
+    testCases: created,
+    execution,
+    links: {
+      crawl: linkCrawl,
+      security: linkSecurity,
+      graphify: linkGraphify,
+      studio: created[0]?.href ?? null,
+    },
+  };
+}
diff --git a/lib/bivia-box.ts b/lib/bivia-box.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d0ed0fd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/bivia-box.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+/**
+ * Bounding-box normalisation for Articul8 Bivia /ground.
+ *
+ * The overlay renders on a fixed 0–1000 canvas, but models return boxes in
+ * three different coordinate spaces: normalized 0–1, Qwen-style 0–1000 milli
+ * pixels, or raw pixels. Keeping this pure module free of Next/Prisma imports
+ * lets both the server route and the parity verifier test every space.
+ */
+export interface NormalisedBox {
+  /** Normalised coordinates in a 0–1000 canvas (overlay-friendly). */
+  x: number;
+  y: number;
+  width: number;
+  height: number;
+  rawBox: [number, number, number, number];
+  space: "normalized" | "milli" | "pixels";
+}
+
+const NOMINAL_VIEWPORT = { width: 1280, height: 720 } as const;
+
+function clampBox(value: number, max: number): number {
+  return Math.round(Math.min(max, Math.max(0, value)) * 10) / 10;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Normalises a model box into a stable 0–1000 canvas while keeping the original
+ * corner coordinates for the floating badge.
+ *
+ *  - `[0..1]` coordinates are treated as normalized.
+ *  - `[0..1000]` coordinates are treated as milli-pixels (Qwen-style).
+ *  - anything larger is treated as pixels on the supplied (or nominal) viewport.
+ *  - `[x, y, w, h]` payloads with an inverted corner are also accepted.
+ */
+export function normalizeGroundBox(
+  raw: number[],
+  viewport: { width: number; height: number } = NOMINAL_VIEWPORT,
+): NormalisedBox {
+  const [a, b, c, d] = raw.map(Number);
+  const rawBox: [number, number, number, number] = [a, b, c, d];
+  const safeA = Number.isFinite(a) ? a : 0;
+  const safeB = Number.isFinite(b) ? b : 0;
+  const safeC = Number.isFinite(c) ? c : 0;
+  const safeD = Number.isFinite(d) ? d : 0;
+  // Corner format [x1,y1,x2,y2] unless inverted, then [x,y,w,h].
+  const x1 = Math.min(safeA, safeC);
+  const y1 = Math.min(safeB, safeD);
+  const x2 = safeC > safeA ? safeC : safeA + Math.max(0, safeC);
+  const y2 = safeD > safeB ? safeD : safeB + Math.max(0, safeD);
+  const magnitude = Math.max(Math.abs(x1), Math.abs(y1), Math.abs(x2), Math.abs(y2));
+
+  let scaleX = 1;
+  let scaleY = 1;
+  let space: NormalisedBox["space"] = "milli";
+  if (magnitude <= 1.5) {
+    scaleX = 1000;
+    scaleY = 1000;
+    space = "normalized";
+  } else if (magnitude > 1000) {
+    const width = viewport.width > 0 ? viewport.width : NOMINAL_VIEWPORT.width;
+    const height = viewport.height > 0 ? viewport.height : NOMINAL_VIEWPORT.height;
+    scaleX = 1000 / width;
+    scaleY = 1000 / height;
+    space = "pixels";
+  }
+
+  const nx1 = clampBox(x1 * scaleX, 1000);
+  const ny1 = clampBox(y1 * scaleY, 1000);
+  const nx2 = clampBox(x2 * scaleX, 1000);
+  const ny2 = clampBox(y2 * scaleY, 1000);
+  return {
+    x: Math.min(nx1, nx2),
+    y: Math.min(ny1, ny2),
+    width: Math.max(0, Math.abs(nx2 - nx1)),
+    height: Math.max(0, Math.abs(ny2 - ny1)),
+    rawBox,
+    space,
+  };
+}

+/**
+ * Articul8 Bivia — persistent token ledger and observatory.
+ *
+ * Every calibrated decision (boolean, choice, ground, score, set) appends one
+ * NDJSON line to `.qasyn-data/bivia-telemetry.json`. The ledger is local-only,
+ * git-ignored and capped so it never grows without bound. The observatory
+ * projects it into the 1B token grant widget used by Billing, Integrations and
+ * the Overview.
+ *
+ * Only derived estimates are stored: no prompt text, DOM excerpt, image bytes
+ * or API key is ever written to disk.
+ */
+import fs from "node:fs";
+import path from "node:path";
+
+export const BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_COUNT = 1_000_000_000;
+export const BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_LABEL = "1,000,000,000";
+
+export type BiviaDecisionKind = "boolean" | "choice" | "ground" | "score" | "set";
+
+export interface BiviaTelemetryEntry {
+  id: string;
+  at: string;
+  kind: BiviaDecisionKind;
+  provider: string;
+  status: "answered" | "abstained";
+  /** Raw calibrated probability in [0,1]. Ground decisions use their confidence. */
+  probability: number;
+  latencyMs: number;
+  inputTokens: number;
+  textTokens: number;
+  imageTokens: number;
+  outputTokens: number;
+  organisationId: string | null;
+  mode: string | null;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaObservatorySummary {
+  grant: number;
+  grantLabel: string;
+  used: number;
+  remaining: number;
+  usedPct: number;
+  decisions: number;
+  counts: Record<BiviaDecisionKind, number>;
+  answered: number;
+  abstained: number;
+  avgConfidencePct: number;
+  abstentionRatePct: number;
+  avgLatencyMs: number;
+  estimatedCostSaved: number;
+  imageTokens: number;
+  textTokens: number;
+  providerSplit: { api: number; fallback: number };
+  lastDecisionAt: string | null;
+}
+
+const MAX_ENTRIES = 5_000;
+const LEDGER_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), ".qasyn-data");
+const LEDGER_PATH = path.join(LEDGER_DIR, "bivia-telemetry.json");
+
+function emptySummary(): BiviaObservatorySummary {
+  return {
+    grant: BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_COUNT,
+    grantLabel: BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_LABEL,
+    used: 0,
+    remaining: BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_COUNT,
+    usedPct: 0,
+    decisions: 0,
+    counts: { boolean: 0, choice: 0, ground: 0, score: 0, set: 0 },
+    answered: 0,
+    abstained: 0,
+    avgConfidencePct: 0,
+    abstentionRatePct: 0,
+    avgLatencyMs: 0,
+    estimatedCostSaved: 0,
+    imageTokens: 0,
+    textTokens: 0,
+    providerSplit: { api: 0, fallback: 0 },
+    lastDecisionAt: null,
+  };
+}
+
+function round1(value: number): number {
+  return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
+}
+
+function round2(value: number): number {
+  return Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
+}
+
+/** Rough but stable token estimate: 4 characters per text token. */
+export function estimateTextTokens(text: string | null | undefined): number {
+  const value = String(text ?? "").trim();
+  if (!value) return 0;
+  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(value.length / 4));
+}
+
+/**
+ * Screenshots are sent as base64 images. A byte is ~1.33 base64 characters and
+ * multimodal image tokens are estimated at one token per 6 base64 characters.
+ */
+export function estimateImageTokens(base64: string | null | undefined): number {
+  const value = String(base64 ?? "");
+  if (!value) return 0;
+  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(value.length / 6));
+}
+
+function safeReadEntries(): BiviaTelemetryEntry[] {
+  try {
+    const raw = fs.readFileSync(LEDGER_PATH, "utf8");
+    const entries: BiviaTelemetryEntry[] = [];
+    for (const line of raw.split(/\r?\n/)) {
+      const trimmed = line.trim();
+      if (!trimmed) continue;
+      try {
+        const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed) as Partial<BiviaTelemetryEntry>;
+        if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || !parsed.kind) continue;
+        entries.push({
+          id: String(parsed.id ?? `${parsed.at ?? ""}-${entries.length}`),
+          at: String(parsed.at ?? new Date().toISOString()),
+          kind: parsed.kind as BiviaDecisionKind,
+          provider: String(parsed.provider ?? "bivia-calibrated-fallback"),
+          status: parsed.status === "abstained" ? "abstained" : "answered",
+          probability: Number(parsed.probability ?? 0) || 0,
+          latencyMs: Number(parsed.latencyMs ?? 0) || 0,
+          inputTokens: Number(parsed.inputTokens ?? 0) || 0,
+          textTokens: Number(parsed.textTokens ?? 0) || 0,
+          imageTokens: Number(parsed.imageTokens ?? 0) || 0,
+          outputTokens: Number(parsed.outputTokens ?? 0) || 0,
+          organisationId: parsed.organisationId ? String(parsed.organisationId) : null,
+          mode: parsed.mode ? String(parsed.mode) : null,
+        });
+      } catch {
+        /* skip malformed line */
+      }
+    }
+    return entries.length > MAX_ENTRIES ? entries.slice(-MAX_ENTRIES) : entries;
+  } catch {
+    return [];
+  }
+}
+
+function compactLedger(entries: BiviaTelemetryEntry[]) {
+  if (entries.length <= MAX_ENTRIES) return;
+  try {
+    const kept = entries.slice(-MAX_ENTRIES);
+    fs.writeFileSync(LEDGER_PATH, `${kept.map((entry) => JSON.stringify(entry)).join("\n")}\n`, "utf8");
+  } catch {
+    /* best-effort compaction */
+  }
+}
+
+export interface RecordBiviaDecisionInput {
+  kind: BiviaDecisionKind;
+  provider: string;
+  status: "answered" | "abstained";
+  probability: number;
+  latencyMs: number;
+  question?: string | null;
+  context?: string | null;
+  screenshotBase64?: string | null;
+  organisationId?: string | null;
+  mode?: string | null;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Appends one decision to the ledger. Never throws: telemetry can never break a
+ * decision path or an HTTP route.
+ */
+export function recordBiviaDecision(input: RecordBiviaDecisionInput): void {
+  const textTokens = estimateTextTokens(`${input.question ?? ""}\n${input.context ?? ""}`);
+  const imageTokens = estimateImageTokens(input.screenshotBase64);
+  const outputTokens = 64;
+  const entry: BiviaTelemetryEntry = {
+    id: `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`,
+    at: new Date().toISOString(),
+    kind: input.kind,
+    provider: input.provider,
+    status: input.status,
+    probability: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(input.probability) || 0)),
+    latencyMs: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(input.latencyMs) || 0)),
+    inputTokens: textTokens + imageTokens,
+    textTokens,
+    imageTokens,
+    outputTokens,
+    organisationId: input.organisationId ?? null,
+    mode: input.mode ?? null,
+  };
+  try {
+    fs.mkdirSync(LEDGER_DIR, { recursive: true });
+    fs.appendFileSync(LEDGER_PATH, `${JSON.stringify(entry)}\n`, "utf8");
+    const entries = safeReadEntries();
+    compactLedger(entries);
+  } catch {
+    /* best-effort telemetry */
+  }
+}
+
+/** Projects the persistent ledger into the observatory summary. */
+export function getBiviaObservatory(organisationId?: string): BiviaObservatorySummary {
+  const allEntries = safeReadEntries();
+  const entries = organisationId ? allEntries.filter((entry) => entry.organisationId === organisationId) : allEntries;
+  if (entries.length === 0) return emptySummary();
+
+  const counts: Record<BiviaDecisionKind, number> = { boolean: 0, choice: 0, ground: 0, score: 0, set: 0 };
+  let used = 0;
+  let textTokens = 0;
+  let imageTokens = 0;
+  let probabilitySum = 0;
+  let latencySum = 0;
+  let answered = 0;
+  let abstained = 0;
+  let api = 0;
+  let fallback = 0;
+  let lastDecisionAt: string | null = null;
+
+  for (const entry of entries) {
+    if (entry.kind in counts) counts[entry.kind] += 1;
+    used += entry.inputTokens + entry.outputTokens;
+    textTokens += entry.textTokens;
+    imageTokens += entry.imageTokens;
+    probabilitySum += entry.probability;
+    latencySum += entry.latencyMs;
+    if (entry.status === "abstained") abstained += 1;
+    else answered += 1;
+    if (entry.provider === "bivia-api") api += 1;
+    else fallback += 1;
+    if (!lastDecisionAt || entry.at > lastDecisionAt) lastDecisionAt = entry.at;
+  }
+
+  const decisions = entries.length;
+  const avgLatencyMs = decisions ? latencySum / decisions : 0;
+  // Estimated avoided third-party multimodal LLM spend: $0.59 / 1M input
+  // tokens + $0.0024 per orchestrated decision. Derived, never billed.
+  const estimatedCostSaved = round2((used / 1_000_000) * 0.59 + decisions * 0.0024);
+
+  return {
+    grant: BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_COUNT,
+    grantLabel: BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_LABEL,
+    used,
+    remaining: Math.max(0, BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_COUNT - used),
+    usedPct: Math.min(100, (used / BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT_COUNT) * 100),
+    decisions,
+    counts,
+    answered,
+    abstained,
+    avgConfidencePct: round1((probabilitySum / Math.max(1, decisions)) * 100),
+    abstentionRatePct: round1((abstained / Math.max(1, decisions)) * 100),
+    avgLatencyMs: Math.round(avgLatencyMs),
+    estimatedCostSaved,
+    imageTokens,
+    textTokens,
+    providerSplit: { api, fallback },
+    lastDecisionAt,
+  };
+}
