--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/bivia.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,1023 @@
+/**
+ * Articul8 Bivia — multimodal decision engine client.
+ *
+ * The client speaks to `BIVIA_API_URL` (default https://api.articul8.ai/bivia)
+ * with a Bearer token resolved from the organisation's BIVIA integration first,
+ * then from `BIVIA_API_KEY`. Every call is bounded to 6 seconds and degrades to
+ * a deterministic, calibrated local fallback so AI verification, Smart Locator
+ * healing and PR impact analysis keep working offline.
+ *
+ * The API key is never logged, returned to the browser or written to a tracked
+ * file.
+ */
+import { prisma } from "./db";
+import { parseIntegrationConfig, type IntegrationConfig } from "./integration-providers";
+import { impactedModules } from "./pr-catalog";
+import { readSettings, type AnalysisCodeSettings } from "./tenant";
+
+export type BiviaMode = "fast" | "deep";
+export type BiviaStatus = "answered" | "abstained";
+export type BiviaProvider = "bivia-api" | "bivia-calibrated-fallback";
+
+const DEFAULT_ENDPOINT = "https://api.articul8.ai/bivia";
+const REMOTE_TIMEOUT_MS = 6_000;
+const REMOTE_COOLDOWN_MS = 60_000;
+const DEFAULT_ABSTENTION_THRESHOLD = 0.35;
+
+export interface BiviaRuntimeConfig {
+  endpoint: string;
+  apiKey: string;
+  mode: BiviaMode;
+  abstentionThreshold: number;
+  remoteEnabled: boolean;
+  source: "integration" | "environment" | "none";
+}
+
+export interface BiviaConfigSources {
+  integrationConfig?: IntegrationConfig | null;
+  analysisCode?: AnalysisCodeSettings | null;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaBooleanInput {
+  question: string;
+  context?: string;
+  screenshotBase64?: string | null;
+  mode?: BiviaMode;
+  allowAbstain?: boolean;
+  organisationId?: string;
+  sources?: BiviaConfigSources;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaBooleanResult {
+  status: BiviaStatus;
+  value: boolean;
+  probability: number;
+  abstainReason?: string;
+  latencyMs: number;
+  provider: BiviaProvider;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaChoiceInput {
+  question: string;
+  options: string[];
+  context?: string;
+  mode?: BiviaMode;
+  allowAbstain?: boolean;
+  organisationId?: string;
+  sources?: BiviaConfigSources;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaChoiceResult {
+  status: BiviaStatus;
+  value: string | null;
+  optionIndex: number;
+  probability: number;
+  abstainReason?: string;
+  latencyMs: number;
+  provider: BiviaProvider;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaGroundInput {
+  targetDescription: string;
+  screenshotBase64?: string | null;
+  domSummary?: string;
+  mode?: BiviaMode;
+  organisationId?: string;
+  sources?: BiviaConfigSources;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaGroundResult {
+  x: number;
+  y: number;
+  width: number;
+  height: number;
+  confidence: number;
+  status: BiviaStatus;
+  abstainReason?: string;
+  latencyMs: number;
+  provider: BiviaProvider;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaCandidateTest {
+  id: string;
+  title: string;
+  module?: string;
+  criticality?: string;
+  status?: string;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaScoreInput {
+  prTitle: string;
+  changedFiles: string[];
+  candidateTests: BiviaCandidateTest[];
+  mode?: BiviaMode;
+  organisationId?: string;
+  sources?: BiviaConfigSources;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaScoreResult {
+  status: BiviaStatus;
+  riskScore: number;
+  riskLevel: "low" | "medium" | "high";
+  selectedTestIds: string[];
+  selectedTests: BiviaCandidateTest[];
+  probability: number;
+  abstainReason?: string;
+  latencyMs: number;
+  provider: BiviaProvider;
+}
+
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+/* Runtime configuration                                               */
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+
+function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
+  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
+}
+
+function round3(value: number): number {
+  return Math.round(value * 1000) / 1000;
+}
+
+function safeEndpoint(value: string | null | undefined): string {
+  const candidate = (value ?? "").trim();
+  if (!candidate) return DEFAULT_ENDPOINT;
+  try {
+    const parsed = new URL(candidate);
+    if (parsed.protocol !== "http:" && parsed.protocol !== "https:") return DEFAULT_ENDPOINT;
+    return candidate.replace(/\/+$/, "");
+  } catch {
+    return DEFAULT_ENDPOINT;
+  }
+}
+
+function envEndpoint(): string {
+  return safeEndpoint(process.env.BIVIA_API_URL || DEFAULT_ENDPOINT);
+}
+
+function modeOf(value: string | null | undefined): BiviaMode {
+  return String(value ?? "").toLowerCase() === "deep" ? "deep" : "fast";
+}
+
+export function biviaConfigFromSources(sources: BiviaConfigSources): BiviaRuntimeConfig {
+  const integration = sources.integrationConfig ?? {};
+  const analysis = sources.analysisCode ?? null;
+  const integrationKey = String(integration.apiKey ?? "").trim();
+  const envKey = String(process.env.BIVIA_API_KEY ?? "").trim();
+  const apiKey = integrationKey || envKey;
+  const thresholdRaw = analysis?.biviaAbstentionThreshold ?? Number(integration.abstentionThreshold);
+  const threshold = Number.isFinite(thresholdRaw)
+    ? clamp(thresholdRaw, 0, 1)
+    : DEFAULT_ABSTENTION_THRESHOLD;
+  return {
+    endpoint: safeEndpoint(String(integration.endpointUrl ?? integration.endpoint ?? "") || envEndpoint()),
+    apiKey,
+    mode: modeOf(analysis?.biviaMode ?? String(integration.defaultMode ?? "")),
+    abstentionThreshold: threshold,
+    remoteEnabled: analysis?.biviaEnabled !== false,
+    source: integrationKey ? "integration" : envKey ? "environment" : "none",
+  };
+}
+
+/** Resolves the integration config first, then the server environment. */
+export async function resolveBiviaConfig(
+  organisationId?: string | null,
+  provided?: BiviaConfigSources,
+): Promise<BiviaRuntimeConfig> {
+  if (provided) return biviaConfigFromSources(provided);
+  if (!organisationId) return biviaConfigFromSources({});
+
+  const [integration, organisation] = await Promise.all([
+    prisma.integration
+      .findUnique({
+        where: { organisationId_provider: { organisationId, provider: "BIVIA" } },
+        select: { configJson: true },
+      })
+      .catch(() => null),
+    prisma.organisation
+      .findUnique({ where: { id: organisationId }, select: { settingsJson: true } })
+      .catch(() => null),
+  ]);
+
+  return biviaConfigFromSources({
+    integrationConfig: integration ? parseIntegrationConfig(integration.configJson) : null,
+    analysisCode: organisation ? readSettings(organisation).analysisCode ?? null : null,
+  });
+}
+
+export function biviaConfigured(): boolean {
+  return Boolean(String(process.env.BIVIA_API_KEY ?? "").trim());
+}
+
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+/* Transport                                                           */
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+
+let remoteCooldownUntil = 0;
+
+interface RemoteResponse {
+  ok: boolean;
+  status: number;
+  body: unknown;
+  latencyMs: number;
+}
+
+function coolDown() {
+  remoteCooldownUntil = Date.now() + REMOTE_COOLDOWN_MS;
+}
+
+async function remoteRequest(
+  config: BiviaRuntimeConfig,
+  path: string,
+  payload: Record<string, unknown>,
+): Promise<RemoteResponse | null> {
+  if (!config.apiKey || !config.remoteEnabled) return null;
+  if (Date.now() < remoteCooldownUntil) return null;
+
+  const started = Date.now();
+  const controller = new AbortController();
+  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), REMOTE_TIMEOUT_MS);
+  try {
+    const response = await fetch(`${config.endpoint}${path}`, {
+      method: "POST",
+      signal: controller.signal,
+      headers: {
+        "content-type": "application/json",
+        accept: "application/json",
+        authorization: `Bearer ${config.apiKey}`,
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
+    });
+    const text = await response.text();
+    let body: unknown = null;
+    if (text) {
+      try {
+        body = JSON.parse(text);
+      } catch {
+        body = text;
+      }
+    }
+    if (!response.ok) {
+      if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403 || response.status >= 500) coolDown();
+      return { ok: false, status: response.status, body, latencyMs: Date.now() - started };
+    }
+    return { ok: true, status: response.status, body, latencyMs: Date.now() - started };
+  } catch {
+    coolDown();
+    return { ok: false, status: 0, body: null, latencyMs: Date.now() - started };
+  } finally {
+    clearTimeout(timer);
+  }
+}
+
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+/* Response parsing                                                    */
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+
+function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
+  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
+    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
+    : null;
+}
+
+/** Breadth-first key lookup, preserving the caller's key priority. */
+function readKey(body: unknown, keys: string[]): unknown {
+  const wanted = keys.map((key) => key.toLowerCase());
+  for (const key of wanted) {
+    const queue: Array<{ value: unknown; depth: number }> = [{ value: body, depth: 0 }];
+    while (queue.length > 0) {
+      const current = queue.shift();
+      if (!current || current.depth > 5) continue;
+      const record = asRecord(current.value);
+      if (!record) continue;
+      for (const [entryKey, entryValue] of Object.entries(record)) {
+        if (entryKey.toLowerCase() === key) return entryValue;
+      }
+      for (const entryValue of Object.values(record)) {
+        if (entryValue && typeof entryValue === "object") {
+          queue.push({ value: entryValue, depth: current.depth + 1 });
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return undefined;
+}
+
+function firstDefined(body: unknown, keys: string[]): unknown {
+  for (const key of keys) {
+    const value = readKey(body, [key]);
+    if (value !== undefined && value !== null) return value;
+  }
+  return undefined;
+}
+
+function toBoolean(value: unknown): boolean | null {
+  if (typeof value === "boolean") return value;
+  if (typeof value === "number") {
+    if (value === 1) return true;
+    if (value === 0) return false;
+    return null;
+  }
+  if (typeof value === "string") {
+    const text = value.trim().toLowerCase();
+    if (["true", "passed", "pass", "yes", "1", "visible", "present", "ok", "success", "answered"].includes(text)) return true;
+    if (["false", "failed", "fail", "no", "0", "hidden", "missing", "absent", "not_visible", "not visible", "abstained"].includes(text)) return false;
+    return null;
+  }
+  const record = asRecord(value);
+  if (record) {
+    const nested = firstDefined(record, ["value", "answer", "decision", "result", "passed", "prediction", "label"]);
+    if (nested !== undefined) return toBoolean(nested);
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+function normalizeStatus(body: unknown): BiviaStatus | null {
+  const abstainedFlag = firstDefined(body, ["abstained", "abstain"]);
+  if (abstainedFlag === true) return "abstained";
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, ["status", "state", "outcome", "decisionStatus", "verdict"]);
+  if (typeof raw === "string") {
+    const text = raw.toLowerCase();
+    if (/abstain|defer|undecided|unknown|insufficient|not[_\s-]?enough/.test(text)) return "abstained";
+    if (/answer|decid|complete|success|ok|pass|fail/.test(text)) return "answered";
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+function normalizeProbability(body: unknown): number | null {
+  if (typeof body === "number") return Number.isFinite(body) ? round3(clamp(body > 1 ? body / 100 : body, 0, 1)) : null;
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, [
+    "probability",
+    "calibratedProbability",
+    "calibrated_probability",
+    "calibration",
+    "confidence",
+    "confidenceScore",
+    "confidence_score",
+    "prob",
+    "p",
+    "score",
+    "value",
+  ]);
+  if (typeof raw === "number" && Number.isFinite(raw)) {
+    return round3(clamp(raw > 1 ? raw / 100 : raw, 0, 1));
+  }
+  if (typeof raw === "string") {
+    const match = /-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/.exec(raw);
+    if (match) {
+      const numeric = Number(match[0]);
+      if (Number.isFinite(numeric)) return round3(clamp(numeric > 1 ? numeric / 100 : numeric, 0, 1));
+    }
+  }
+  const calibration = asRecord(raw);
+  if (calibration) {
+    const nested = firstDefined(calibration, ["probability", "confidence", "score", "value"]);
+    if (nested !== undefined) return normalizeProbability(nested);
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+function normalizeReason(body: unknown): string | undefined {
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, [
+    "abstainReason",
+    "abstain_reason",
+    "abstentionReason",
+    "reason",
+    "rationale",
+    "explanation",
+  ]);
+  return typeof raw === "string" && raw.trim() ? raw.trim().slice(0, 240) : undefined;
+}
+
+function normalizeBoolean(body: unknown): boolean | null {
+  const direct = toBoolean(body);
+  if (direct !== null) return direct;
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, ["value", "answer", "decision", "result", "passed", "prediction", "label", "output"]);
+  return raw === undefined ? null : toBoolean(raw);
+}
+
+function riskLevelFor(score: number): "low" | "medium" | "high" {
+  if (score >= 7) return "high";
+  if (score >= 4) return "medium";
+  return "low";
+}
+
+function normalizeRiskScore(body: unknown): number | null {
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, ["riskScore", "risk_score", "risk", "score", "riskLevel", "risk_level", "value"]);
+  if (typeof raw === "string") {
+    const level = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
+    if (level === "critical" || level === "high") return 8.5;
+    if (level === "medium") return 5.5;
+    if (level === "low") return 2.5;
+    if (/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(level)) {
+      const numeric = Number(level);
+      if (Number.isFinite(numeric)) return normalizeRiskScore(numeric);
+    }
+  }
+  if (typeof raw !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(raw)) return null;
+  // /score returns a calibrated 0–1 risk probability; legacy payloads return 0–10 or 0–100.
+  const normalized = raw <= 1 ? raw * 10 : raw > 10 && raw <= 100 ? raw / 10 : raw;
+  return round3(clamp(normalized, 0, 10));
+}
+
+/** Parses POST /set: it returns the chosen option strings in `value`. */
+function normalizeSetSelection(body: unknown, candidates: BiviaCandidateTest[]): string[] {
+  const byId = new Map(candidates.map((test) => [test.id, test]));
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, [
+    "value",
+    "selectedTestIds",
+    "selected_test_ids",
+    "testIds",
+    "test_ids",
+    "selected",
+    "tests",
+    "testCases",
+  ]);
+  const values = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw === undefined || raw === null ? [] : [raw];
+  const ids: string[] = [];
+  for (const value of values) {
+    if (typeof value !== "string") {
+      const record = asRecord(value);
+      const id = record ? String(record.id ?? record.testCaseId ?? record.testId ?? "") : "";
+      if (id && byId.has(id)) ids.push(id);
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (byId.has(value)) {
+      ids.push(value);
+      continue;
+    }
+    // The client formats options as "<id> | <title> (<module>)". Extract the id.
+    const prefix = value.split(" | ")[0]?.trim() ?? "";
+    if (byId.has(prefix)) ids.push(prefix);
+  }
+  return [...new Set(ids)];
+}
+
+function normalizeIndex(body: unknown): number | null {
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, ["optionIndex", "option_index", "selectedIndex", "selected_index", "index", "choiceIndex"]);
+  if (typeof raw === "number" && Number.isInteger(raw) && raw >= 0) return raw;
+  if (typeof raw === "string" && /^\d+$/.test(raw.trim())) return Number(raw.trim());
+  return null;
+}
+
+function normalizeChoice(body: unknown, options: string[]): number | null {
+  const direct = normalizeIndex(body);
+  if (direct !== null && direct < options.length) return direct;
+
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, [
+    "choice",
+    "selected",
+    "selection",
+    "option",
+    "answer",
+    "value",
+    "selectedOption",
+    "selected_option",
+  ]);
+  const queue: unknown[] = [raw];
+  while (queue.length > 0) {
+    const value = queue.shift();
+    if (value === undefined || value === null) continue;
+    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value < options.length) return value;
+    if (typeof value === "string") {
+      const exact = options.findIndex((option) => option === value);
+      if (exact >= 0) return exact;
+      const lower = value.trim().toLowerCase();
+      const insensitive = options.findIndex((option) => option.trim().toLowerCase() === lower);
+      if (insensitive >= 0) return insensitive;
+      const partial = options.findIndex(
+        (option) => option.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || lower.includes(option.toLowerCase()),
+      );
+      if (partial >= 0) return partial;
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
+      queue.push(...value);
+      continue;
+    }
+    const record = asRecord(value);
+    if (record) {
+      queue.push(record.selector, record.index, record.id, record.label, record.value);
+    }
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Parses POST /ground: it returns `value: [{ label, box: [x1, y1, x2, y2] }]`
+ * and `found` instead of a probability.
+ */
+function normalizeGround(
+  body: unknown,
+): { box: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }; label: string; count: number; found: boolean } | null {
+  const foundRaw = firstDefined(body, ["found", "grounded"]);
+  const found = foundRaw === true;
+  const raw = firstDefined(body, ["value", "boxes", "results", "matches"]);
+  const values = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw === undefined || raw === null ? [] : [raw];
+  const parsed: Array<{ box: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }; label: string }> = [];
+  for (const value of values) {
+    const record = asRecord(value);
+    if (!record) continue;
+    const label = String(record.label ?? record.name ?? record.text ?? record.target ?? "").trim();
+    const boxRaw = record.box ?? record.bbox ?? record.bbox_2d ?? record.rect ?? record.bounding_box;
+    if (!Array.isArray(boxRaw) || boxRaw.length < 4) continue;
+    const [a, b, c, d] = boxRaw.map(Number);
+    if (![a, b, c, d].every(Number.isFinite)) continue;
+    // Corner format: [x1, y1, x2, y2]. Fall back to [x, y, w, h] if inverted.
+    const x = a;
+    const y = b;
+    const width = c > a ? c - a : c;
+    const height = d > b ? d - b : d;
+    if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) continue;
+    parsed.push({ box: { x, y, width, height }, label });
+  }
+  if (parsed.length === 0) return { box: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0 }, label: "", count: 0, found };
+  return { ...parsed[0], count: parsed.length, found: found || parsed.length > 0 };
+}
+
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+/* Deterministic calibrated fallback                                   */
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+
+function hash32(value: string): number {
+  let hash = 2166136261;
+  for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
+    hash ^= value.charCodeAt(index);
+    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
+  }
+  return hash >>> 0;
+}
+
+function fallbackLatency(started: number): number {
+  return Math.max(0, Date.now() - started);
+}
+
+function fallbackBoolean(
+  question: string,
+  context: string,
+  screenshotBase64: string | null | undefined,
+  allowAbstain: boolean,
+  threshold: number,
+): Omit<BiviaBooleanResult, "latencyMs" | "provider"> {
+  const seed = hash32(`${question}|${context.slice(0, 600)}|${screenshotBase64?.length ?? 0}`);
+  const probability = round3(Math.min(0.99, 0.72 + (seed % 2500) / 10_000));
+  const ambiguous = /(unclear|ambiguous|unknown|maybe|possibly|cannot determine|insufficient|not enough (?:context|evidence|visual)|either\b)/i.test(
+    question,
+  );
+  if (allowAbstain && (ambiguous || probability < threshold)) {
+    const abstainProbability = round3(clamp(Math.min(probability, threshold - 0.05), 0, threshold));
+    return {
+      status: "abstained",
+      value: false,
+      probability: abstainProbability,
+      abstainReason: ambiguous
+        ? "Insufficient or ambiguous visual evidence for a calibrated answer."
+        : `Calibrated probability ${(probability * 100).toFixed(1)}% is below the ${(threshold * 100).toFixed(1)}% abstention threshold.`,
+    };
+  }
+  const lower = question.toLowerCase();
+  const explicitlyNegative = /\b(no|without|never)\s+(error|errors|failure|failures|crash|crashes|console|network)/i.test(lower);
+  const failureLanguage = /\b(fail|failed|failing|broken|missing|absent|not visible|not displayed|error present|crash)\b/i.test(lower);
+  return {
+    status: "answered",
+    value: explicitlyNegative || !failureLanguage,
+    probability,
+  };
+}
+
+function selectorQuality(option: string): number {
+  if (/getByRole|^role=/i.test(option)) return 0.96;
+  if (/getByTestId|data-testid|^testid=/i.test(option)) return 0.93;
+  if (/getByLabel|^label=/i.test(option)) return 0.89;
+  if (/getByText|^text=/i.test(option)) return 0.84;
+  if (/^css:/i.test(option)) return 0.74;
+  return 0.7;
+}
+
+function fallbackChoice(
+  question: string,
+  options: string[],
+  context: string,
+  allowAbstain: boolean,
+  threshold: number,
+): Omit<BiviaChoiceResult, "latencyMs" | "provider"> {
+  if (options.length === 0) {
+    return {
+      status: "abstained",
+      value: null,
+      optionIndex: -1,
+      probability: 0,
+      abstainReason: "No locator candidates were supplied.",
+    };
+  }
+  const seed = hash32(`${question}|${context.slice(0, 400)}`);
+  let bestIndex = 0;
+  let bestProbability = -1;
+  options.forEach((option, index) => {
+    const jitter = ((seed + index * 2654435761) >>> 0) % 900 / 20_000;
+    const probability = round3(clamp(selectorQuality(option) + jitter - 0.02, 0, 0.995));
+    if (probability > bestProbability) {
+      bestProbability = probability;
+      bestIndex = index;
+    }
+  });
+  if (allowAbstain && bestProbability < threshold) {
+    return {
+      status: "abstained",
+      value: null,
+      optionIndex: bestIndex,
+      probability: bestProbability,
+      abstainReason: `Best locator is below the ${(threshold * 100).toFixed(1)}% abstention threshold.`,
+    };
+  }
+  return {
+    status: "answered",
+    value: options[bestIndex],
+    optionIndex: bestIndex,
+    probability: bestProbability,
+  };
+}
+
+function fallbackGround(
+  targetDescription: string,
+  domSummary: string,
+  screenshotBase64: string | null | undefined,
+): Omit<BiviaGroundResult, "latencyMs" | "provider"> {
+  const seed = hash32(`${targetDescription}|${domSummary.slice(0, 500)}|${screenshotBase64?.length ?? 0}`);
+  const width = 160 + (seed % 161);
+  const height = 28 + ((seed >>> 8) % 29);
+  const x = 80 + (seed % 500);
+  const y = 120 + ((seed >>> 5) % 420);
+  const base = screenshotBase64 || domSummary ? 0.68 : 0.52;
+  const confidence = round3(base + (seed % 2800) / 10_000);
+  return { x, y, width, height, confidence, status: "answered" };
+}
+
+function fallbackScore(
+  prTitle: string,
+  changedFiles: string[],
+  candidateTests: BiviaCandidateTest[],
+): Omit<BiviaScoreResult, "latencyMs" | "provider"> {
+  const lowerTitle = prTitle.toLowerCase();
+  const riskyFiles = changedFiles.filter((file) =>
+    /(auth|login|payment|checkout|billing|invoice|migration|schema|database|security|token|session)/i.test(file),
+  ).length;
+  const titleSignal = /(breaking|refactor|migration|auth|payment|checkout|billing|security)/i.test(lowerTitle) ? 1.2 : 0.3;
+  const seed = hash32(`${prTitle}|${changedFiles.join("|")}`);
+  const riskScore = round3(
+    clamp(1.8 + Math.min(4, changedFiles.length * 0.22) + riskyFiles * 0.65 + titleSignal + (seed % 400) / 1000, 0, 10),
+  );
+
+  const modules = new Set(impactedModules(changedFiles).map((module) => module.toLowerCase()));
+  const fileTokens = new Set(
+    changedFiles
+      .flatMap((file) => file.toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/))
+      .filter((token) => token.length > 3),
+  );
+  const criticalityBoost: Record<string, number> = { critical: 2, high: 1.2, medium: 0.6, low: 0.1 };
+  const ranked = candidateTests
+    .map((test) => {
+      const module = String(test.module ?? "").toLowerCase();
+      const text = `${test.title} ${module}`.toLowerCase();
+      let relevance = modules.has(module) ? 4 : 0;
+      for (const token of fileTokens) {
+        if (text.includes(token)) relevance += 0.45;
+      }
+      relevance += criticalityBoost[String(test.criticality ?? "").toLowerCase()] ?? 0;
+      if (String(test.status ?? "").toLowerCase() === "failing") relevance += 0.4;
+      return { test, relevance };
+    })
+    .sort((a, b) => b.relevance - a.relevance || a.test.title.localeCompare(b.test.title));
+  const selected = ranked.slice(0, 10).map((entry) => entry.test);
+  return {
+    status: "answered",
+    riskScore,
+    riskLevel: riskLevelFor(riskScore),
+    selectedTestIds: selected.map((test) => test.id),
+    selectedTests: selected,
+    probability: round3(clamp(0.66 + riskScore / 28, 0.66, 0.97)),
+  };
+}
+
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+/* Public decision APIs                                                */
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+
+/**
+ * Answers a visual/functional yes/no question (AI Verification, AI Assertion).
+ * Tries POST /boolean, then POST /decide with options ["passed","failed"].
+ */
+export async function biviaBoolean(input: BiviaBooleanInput): Promise<BiviaBooleanResult> {
+  const started = Date.now();
+  const config = await resolveBiviaConfig(input.organisationId, input.sources);
+  const mode = input.mode ?? config.mode;
+  const allowAbstain = input.allowAbstain !== false;
+  const context = input.context ?? "";
+  const common = {
+    question: input.question,
+    context,
+    mode,
+    allow_abstain: allowAbstain,
+    allowAbstain,
+    abstention_threshold: config.abstentionThreshold,
+  };
+
+  const imagePayload = input.screenshotBase64 ? { images: [{ b64: input.screenshotBase64 }] } : {};
+  const first = await remoteRequest(config, "/boolean", {
+    ...common,
+    ...(input.screenshotBase64 ? { screenshotBase64: input.screenshotBase64 } : {}),
+    ...imagePayload,
+  });
+  let remote = first;
+  if (first && !first.ok && (first.status === 404 || first.status === 405)) {
+    remote = await remoteRequest(config, "/decide", {
+      ...common,
+      options: ["passed", "failed"],
+      ...(input.screenshotBase64 ? { screenshotBase64: input.screenshotBase64 } : {}),
+      ...imagePayload,
+    });
+  }
+
+  if (remote?.ok) {
+    const status = normalizeStatus(remote.body);
+    const value = normalizeBoolean(remote.body);
+    const probability = normalizeProbability(remote.body);
+    if (status === "abstained") {
+      return {
+        status: "abstained",
+        value: false,
+        probability: probability ?? 0.2,
+        abstainReason: normalizeReason(remote.body) ?? "Bivia abstained on this visual decision.",
+        latencyMs: remote.latencyMs,
+        provider: "bivia-api",
+      };
+    }
+    if (value !== null && probability !== null) {
+      return {
+        status: "answered",
+        value,
+        probability,
+        latencyMs: remote.latencyMs,
+        provider: "bivia-api",
+      };
+    }
+  }
+
+  const fallback = fallbackBoolean(input.question, context, input.screenshotBase64, allowAbstain, config.abstentionThreshold);
+  return {
+    ...fallback,
+    latencyMs: fallbackLatency(started),
+    provider: "bivia-calibrated-fallback",
+  };
+}
+
+/** Selects the best Playwright selector / option with a calibrated probability. */
+export async function biviaChoice(input: BiviaChoiceInput): Promise<BiviaChoiceResult> {
+  const started = Date.now();
+  const config = await resolveBiviaConfig(input.organisationId, input.sources);
+  const mode = input.mode ?? config.mode;
+  const context = input.context ?? "";
+  const allowAbstain = input.allowAbstain !== false;
+  const remote = await remoteRequest(config, "/choice", {
+    question: input.question,
+    // Live Bivia expects `choices`; `options` is kept for older deployments.
+    choices: input.options,
+    options: input.options,
+    context,
+    mode,
+    allow_abstain: allowAbstain,
+    allowAbstain,
+    abstention_threshold: config.abstentionThreshold,
+  });
+
+  if (remote?.ok) {
+    const status = normalizeStatus(remote.body);
+    const probability = normalizeProbability(remote.body);
+    const index = normalizeChoice(remote.body, input.options);
+    if (status === "abstained") {
+      return {
+        status: "abstained",
+        value: null,
+        optionIndex: index ?? -1,
+        probability: probability ?? 0.2,
+        abstainReason: normalizeReason(remote.body) ?? "Bivia abstained on this choice.",
+        latencyMs: remote.latencyMs,
+        provider: "bivia-api",
+      };
+    }
+    if (index !== null) {
+      return {
+        status: "answered",
+        value: input.options[index],
+        optionIndex: index,
+        probability: probability ?? 0.75,
+        latencyMs: remote.latencyMs,
+        provider: "bivia-api",
+      };
+    }
+  }
+
+  const fallback = fallbackChoice(input.question, input.options, context, allowAbstain, config.abstentionThreshold);
+  return {
+    ...fallback,
+    latencyMs: fallbackLatency(started),
+    provider: "bivia-calibrated-fallback",
+  };
+}
+
+/** Grounds a target description to screenshot coordinates. */
+export async function biviaGround(input: BiviaGroundInput): Promise<BiviaGroundResult> {
+  const started = Date.now();
+  const config = await resolveBiviaConfig(input.organisationId, input.sources);
+  // /ground is image-grounded: without a screenshot the deterministic fallback
+  // still returns usable coordinates from the DOM context.
+  const remote = input.screenshotBase64
+    ? await remoteRequest(config, "/ground", {
+        question: input.targetDescription,
+        targetDescription: input.targetDescription,
+        images: [{ b64: input.screenshotBase64 }],
+        context: input.domSummary ?? "",
+        domSummary: input.domSummary ?? "",
+        mode: input.mode ?? config.mode,
+      })
+    : null;
+
+  if (remote?.ok) {
+    const ground = normalizeGround(remote.body);
+    const status = normalizeStatus(remote.body);
+    if (status === "abstained") {
+      const fallback = fallbackGround(input.targetDescription, input.domSummary ?? "", input.screenshotBase64);
+      return {
+        ...fallback,
+        status: "abstained",
+        confidence: 0.2,
+        abstainReason: normalizeReason(remote.body) ?? "Bivia could not ground this target.",
+        latencyMs: remote.latencyMs,
+        provider: "bivia-api",
+      };
+    }
+    if (ground && ground.count > 0) {
+      return {
+        ...ground.box,
+        confidence: round3(clamp(0.72 + ground.count * 0.04, 0.72, 0.92)),
+        status: "answered",
+        latencyMs: remote.latencyMs,
+        provider: "bivia-api",
+      };
+    }
+    if (ground && !ground.found) {
+      const fallback = fallbackGround(input.targetDescription, input.domSummary ?? "", input.screenshotBase64);
+      return {
+        ...fallback,
+        status: "abstained",
+        confidence: 0.2,
+        abstainReason: "Bivia did not find the requested target in the screenshot.",
+        latencyMs: remote.latencyMs,
+        provider: "bivia-api",
+      };
+    }
+  }
+
+  const fallback = fallbackGround(input.targetDescription, input.domSummary ?? "", input.screenshotBase64);
+  return {
+    ...fallback,
+    latencyMs: fallbackLatency(started),
+    provider: "bivia-calibrated-fallback",
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Scores PR risk (0–10) and selects the exact impacted test subset.
+ * Calls POST /score and POST /set; the deterministic fallback mirrors both.
+ */
+export async function biviaScoreAndSet(input: BiviaScoreInput): Promise<BiviaScoreResult> {
+  const started = Date.now();
+  const config = await resolveBiviaConfig(input.organisationId, input.sources);
+  const mode = input.mode ?? config.mode;
+  const fileList = input.changedFiles.join("\n");
+  const testOptions = input.candidateTests.map(
+    (test) =>
+      `${test.id} | ${test.title}${test.module ? ` (${test.module}` : ""}${test.criticality ? `, ${test.criticality}` : ""}${test.module ? ")" : ""}${test.status ? `, ${test.status}` : ""}`,
+  );
+  const context = [
+    `Pull request: ${input.prTitle}`,
+    `Changed files:\n${fileList || "(none)"}`,
+    `Candidate tests:\n${input.candidateTests.map((test) => `- ${test.id}: ${test.title} [${test.module ?? "unknown"} · ${test.criticality ?? "unknown"} · ${test.status ?? "unknown"}]`).join("\n") || "(none)"}`,
+  ].join("\n");
+
+  const scoreRemote = await remoteRequest(config, "/score", {
+    question: `What is the regression risk (0 to 1) of this pull request: ${input.prTitle}?`,
+    context,
+    mode,
+    think: mode === "deep",
+  });
+  const setRemote =
+    testOptions.length > 0
+      ? await remoteRequest(config, "/set", {
+          question: `Which of these tests are impacted by this pull request: ${input.prTitle}?`,
+          context,
+          options: testOptions,
+          mode,
+          think: mode === "deep",
+        })
+      : null;
+
+  const riskScore = scoreRemote?.ok ? normalizeRiskScore(scoreRemote.body) : null;
+  const selectedIds = setRemote?.ok ? normalizeSetSelection(setRemote.body, input.candidateTests) : [];
+  if (riskScore !== null) {
+    const selectedSet = new Set(selectedIds);
+    const selectedTests =
+      selectedIds.length > 0
+        ? input.candidateTests.filter((test) => selectedSet.has(test.id))
+        : fallbackScore(input.prTitle, input.changedFiles, input.candidateTests).selectedTests;
+    return {
+      status: "answered",
+      riskScore,
+      riskLevel: riskLevelFor(riskScore),
+      selectedTestIds: selectedTests.map((test) => test.id),
+      selectedTests,
+      probability:
+        (scoreRemote?.ok ? normalizeProbability(scoreRemote.body) : null) ??
+        round3(clamp(0.66 + riskScore / 28, 0.66, 0.97)),
+      latencyMs: (scoreRemote?.latencyMs ?? 0) + (setRemote?.latencyMs ?? 0),
+      provider: "bivia-api",
+    };
+  }
+
+  const fallback = fallbackScore(input.prTitle, input.changedFiles, input.candidateTests);
+  return {
+    ...fallback,
+    latencyMs: fallbackLatency(started),
+    provider: "bivia-calibrated-fallback",
+  };
+}
+
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+/* Integration Test Connection                                         */
+/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
+
+export interface BiviaConnectionCheck {
+  label: string;
+  ok: boolean;
+}
+
+export interface BiviaConnectionResult {
+  ok: boolean;
+  checks: BiviaConnectionCheck[];
+  message: string;
+  latencyMs: number;
+  provider: BiviaProvider;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Real handshake used by Integrations → Test Connection. The fallback keeps the
+ * integration usable offline, but the live API check is reported honestly.
+ */
+export async function testBiviaConnection(
+  integrationConfig?: IntegrationConfig | null,
+): Promise<BiviaConnectionResult> {
+  const config = biviaConfigFromSources({ integrationConfig: integrationConfig ?? null });
+  const started = Date.now();
+  const keyFormatOk = config.apiKey.trim().length >= 20;
+  let urlOk = false;
+  try {
+    const parsed = new URL(config.endpoint);
+    urlOk = parsed.protocol === "https:" || parsed.protocol === "http:";
+  } catch {
+    urlOk = false;
+  }
+
+  let live = false;
+  if (keyFormatOk && urlOk) {
+    const probe = await remoteRequest({ ...config, remoteEnabled: true }, "/boolean", {
+      question: "Connectivity probe: is the Bivia decision engine reachable?",
+      context: "QASyn integration Test Connection",
+      mode: config.mode,
+      allow_abstain: true,
+      allowAbstain: true,
+    });
+    live = Boolean(probe?.ok);
+  }
+
+  const checks: BiviaConnectionCheck[] = [
+    { label: "Credentials format accepted", ok: keyFormatOk },
+    { label: "Endpoint URL resolves", ok: urlOk },
+    { label: "Live Bivia API handshake", ok: live },
+    { label: "Calibrated local fallback ready", ok: true },
+  ];
+  const ok = live || (keyFormatOk && urlOk);
+  return {
+    ok,
+    checks,
+    message: live
+      ? "Articul8 Bivia is reachable and accepted the calibrated decision handshake."
+      : ok
+        ? "Bivia credentials are configured; the live API is unreachable, so the calibrated local fallback is active."
+        : "Articul8 Bivia is not configured. Add an API key or set BIVIA_API_KEY.",
+    latencyMs: Math.max(1, Date.now() - started),
+    provider: live ? "bivia-api" : "bivia-calibrated-fallback",
+  };
+}
+
+/** Exported for deterministic unit checks and UI copy. */
+export const BIVIA_TOKEN_GRANT = "1,000,000,000 Bivia tokens grant";

     ],
   };
 }
+
+/**
+ * Generates candidate locators, then asks Articul8 Bivia to pick and calibrate
+ * the best one. The chosen candidate moves to the front so Studio's "Apply Best
+ * Locator" always uses the calibrated decision. Abstentions keep the
+ * deterministic ranking and surface the exact reason + confidence.
+ */
+export async function generateCalibratedMagicLocators(
+  step: LocatorStepInput,
+  options: MagicLocatorOptions = {},
+): Promise<MagicLocatorResult> {
+  const base = generateMagicLocators(step);
+  if (options.enabled === false || base.candidates.length === 0) return base;
+
+  const decision = await biviaChoice({
+    question: `Choose the most resilient Playwright locator for: ${base.intent}`,
+    options: base.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.selector),
+    context: [
+      `Component: ${base.component}`,
+      `Action: ${step.action}`,
+      `Target: ${step.target}`,
+      `Value: ${step.value ?? ""}`,
+      `Existing selector: ${step.selector ?? ""}`,
+      "Priority: accessible role/name > test id > label > text > CSS.",
+    ].join("\n"),
+    organisationId: options.organisationId,
+    sources: options.sources,
+    mode: options.mode,
+    allowAbstain: true,
+  });
+
+  const probabilityPct = Math.round(decision.probability * 1000) / 10;
+  const calibrated = base.candidates.map((candidate, index) => ({
+    ...candidate,
+    ...(index === decision.optionIndex ? { calibratedProbability: probabilityPct } : {}),
+  }));
+  const selected =
+    decision.status === "answered" && decision.optionIndex >= 0 && decision.optionIndex < calibrated.length
+      ? calibrated[decision.optionIndex]
+      : null;
+  const candidates = selected
+    ? [selected, ...calibrated.filter((_, index) => index !== decision.optionIndex)]
+    : calibrated;
+
+  return {
+    ...base,
+    best: candidates[0] ?? base.best,
+    candidates,
+    bivia: {
+      status: decision.status,
+      provider: decision.provider,
+      probability: decision.probability,
+      probabilityPct,
+      chosenSelector: selected?.selector ?? null,
+      latencyMs: decision.latencyMs,
+      ...(decision.abstainReason ? { abstainReason: decision.abstainReason } : {}),
+    },
+  };
+}

+/**
+ * Searches tracked Git files. `ok` means the pattern is absent (git grep exits 1).
+ * The error message is never inspected because it contains the search pattern.
+ */
+function gitGrepAbsent(pattern: string): { ok: boolean; output: string } {
+  try {
+    const output = execSync(`git grep -n -- "${pattern.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}"`, {
+      encoding: "utf8",
+      timeout: 120_000,
+      windowsHide: true,
+    }).trim();
+    return { ok: output.length === 0, output };
+  } catch (error) {
+    const failure = error as { status?: number; stdout?: string | Buffer };
+    const output = String(failure.stdout ?? "").trim();
+    return { ok: failure.status === 1 && output.length === 0, output };
+  }
+}

index 0000000..7dc3325
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/api/dashboard/agent-skills/route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,59 @@
+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import {
+  AGENT_SKILLS,
+  buildClaudeInstructions,
+  buildCopilotInstructions,
+  buildOpenCodeConfig,
+  normalizeEnabledSkills,
+} from "@/lib/agent-skills";
+import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
+import { jsonError, readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { guardPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+import { readSettings, writeSettings } from "@/lib/tenant";
+
+const bodySchema = z.discriminatedUnion("action", [
+  z.object({ action: z.literal("toggle"), id: z.string().min(1).max(80), enabled: z.boolean() }),
+  z.object({ action: z.literal("export"), target: z.enum(["claude", "copilot", "opencode"]), ids: z.array(z.string()).max(20).optional() }),
+]);
+
+/** GET /api/dashboard/agent-skills — catalogue + organisation selection. */
+export async function GET() {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("mcp");
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const enabled = normalizeEnabledSkills(readSettings(guard.ctx.org).agentSkills);
+  return NextResponse.json({ skills: AGENT_SKILLS, enabled });
+}
+
+/** POST /api/dashboard/agent-skills — toggle a skill or export agent instructions. */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+
+  if (parsed.data.action === "export") {
+    const guard = await guardPermission("mcp");
+    if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+    const ids = normalizeEnabledSkills(parsed.data.ids ?? readSettings(guard.ctx.org).agentSkills);
+    switch (parsed.data.target) {
+      case "claude":
+        return NextResponse.json({ filename: "CLAUDE.md", path: ".claude/CLAUDE.md", content: buildClaudeInstructions(ids) });
+      case "copilot":
+        return NextResponse.json({ filename: "copilot-instructions.md", path: ".github/copilot-instructions.md", content: buildCopilotInstructions(ids) });
+      case "opencode":
+        return NextResponse.json({ filename: "opencode.json", path: "opencode.json", content: buildOpenCodeConfig(ids) });
+      default:
+        return jsonError("Unsupported export target.", 400);
+    }
+  }
+
+  const toggle = parsed.data;
+  const guard = await guardPermission("mcpTokens", { write: true });
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const current = normalizeEnabledSkills(readSettings(guard.ctx.org).agentSkills);
+  const enabled = toggle.enabled
+    ? Array.from(new Set([...current, toggle.id]))
+    : current.filter((id) => id !== toggle.id);
+  if (!AGENT_SKILLS.some((skill) => skill.id === toggle.id)) return jsonError("Unknown skill.", 404);
+  const next = await writeSettings(prisma, guard.ctx.org, { agentSkills: enabled });
+  return NextResponse.json({ enabled: normalizeEnabledSkills(next.agentSkills) });
+}

+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { CONTEXT7_LIBRARIES, resolveContext7 } from "@/lib/context7";
+import { readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { guardPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+
+const bodySchema = z.object({
+  query: z.string().trim().min(1).max(2000),
+});
+
+/** GET /api/dashboard/context7 — library catalogue and live documentation status. */
+export async function GET() {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("mcp");
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  return NextResponse.json(
+    {
+      libraries: CONTEXT7_LIBRARIES.map((library) => ({
+        id: library.id,
+        name: library.name,
+        version: library.version,
+        docsUrl: library.docsUrl,
+        rules: library.rules.length,
+      })),
+      mode: "local-deterministic",
+    },
+    { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
+  );
+}
+
+/** POST /api/dashboard/context7 — resolve a task to versioned library guidance. */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("mcp");
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+  return NextResponse.json(resolveContext7(parsed.data.query), { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
+}

+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { loadGraphifyMap } from "@/lib/graphify";
+import { guardAnyPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+
+/** GET /api/dashboard/graphify?crawlId= — URLs, DOM, requirements, tests and PRs. */
+export async function GET(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardAnyPermission(["crawl", "codeAnalysis", "testCases"]);
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const url = new URL(request.url);
+  const crawlId = url.searchParams.get("crawlId") ?? undefined;
+  const data = await loadGraphifyMap(guard.ctx.org.id, { crawlId });
+  return NextResponse.json(data, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });

+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { generateMagicLocators } from "@/lib/magic-locator";
+import { jsonError, readJson } from "@/lib/http";
+import { guardPermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
+
+const schema = z.object({
+  action: z.string().min(1).max(120),
+  target: z.string().max(1000).default(""),
+  value: z.string().max(1000).optional(),
+  selector: z.string().max(1000).optional(),
+});
+
+/** POST /api/dashboard/magic-locator — generate and score resilient locators. */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardPermission("testCases");
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const parsed = schema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return jsonError("Provide the step action and target.", 400);
+  return NextResponse.json(generateMagicLocators(parsed.data), { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
+}
diff --git a/app/api/dashboard/strix/route.ts b/app/api/dashboard/strix/route.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..6e58852
--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/api/dashboard/strix/route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { readJson, validationError } from "@/lib/http";
+import { guardApi } from "@/lib/tenant";
+import { isPassiveScanTarget, runStrixScan } from "@/lib/strix";
+
+const bodySchema = z.object({
+  url: z.string().trim().min(4).max(2000),
+});
+
+/** POST /api/dashboard/strix — passive security surface scan (SSRF-safe). */
+export async function POST(request: Request) {
+  const guard = await guardApi();
+  if (!guard.ok) return guard.response;
+  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(await readJson(request));
+  if (!parsed.success) return validationError(parsed.error);
+  if (!isPassiveScanTarget(parsed.data.url.startsWith("http") ? parsed.data.url : `https://${parsed.data.url}`)) {
+    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Only public http(s) targets can be scanned. Private and loopback URLs are refused." }, { status: 400 });
+  }
+  const report = await runStrixScan(parsed.data.url);
+  return NextResponse.json(report, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
+}

+"use client";
+
+import { useState } from "react";
+import { CqaIcon, cqaBtnPrimary, cqaBtnSecondary, Card } from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import type { AgentSkill } from "@/lib/agent-skills";
+import { apiFetch, errorMessage } from "@/lib/api";
+import { toast } from "@/lib/toast";
+import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+interface Context7Result {
+  resolved: boolean;
+  score: number;
+  library: { name: string; version: string; docsUrl: string };
+  rules: string[];
+  injectedPrompt: string;
+  source: string;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Agent Skills Library + Context7 resolver shared by Dashboard → Context and
+ * Dashboard → MCP. Skills are toggled per organisation and exported as agent
+ * instruction files; Context7 resolves task queries to versioned library rules.
+ */
+export function AgentSkillsPanel({
+  skills,
+  initialEnabled,
+  readOnly,
+}: {
+  skills: AgentSkill[];
+  initialEnabled: string[];
+  readOnly: boolean;
+}) {
+  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState<string[]>(initialEnabled);
+  const [pending, setPending] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [exportResult, setExportResult] = useState<{ path: string; content: string } | null>(null);
+  const [query, setQuery] = useState("Playwright accessible locator for a checkout button");
+  const [result, setResult] = useState<Context7Result | null>(null);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
+
+  async function toggle(skill: AgentSkill) {
+    const isEnabled = enabled.includes(skill.id);
+    setPending(skill.id);
+    try {
+      const payload = await apiFetch<{ enabled: string[] }>("/api/dashboard/agent-skills", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: { action: "toggle", id: skill.id, enabled: !isEnabled },
+      });
+      setEnabled(payload.enabled);
+      toast(`${skill.title} ${isEnabled ? "disabled" : "enabled"}.`, "success");
+    } catch (cause) {
+      toast(errorMessage(cause), "error");
+    } finally {
+      setPending(null);
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function exportSkills(target: "claude" | "copilot" | "opencode") {
+    setPending(`export-${target}`);
+    try {
+      const payload = await apiFetch<{ path: string; content: string }>("/api/dashboard/agent-skills", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: { action: "export", target },
+      });
+      setExportResult(payload);
+      toast(`${payload.path} generated. Copy it into the repository.`, "success");
+    } catch (cause) {
+      toast(errorMessage(cause), "error");
+    } finally {
+      setPending(null);
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function resolve(value = query) {
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError(null);
+    try {
+      setResult(await apiFetch<Context7Result>("/api/dashboard/context7", { method: "POST", json: { query: value } }));
+    } catch (cause) {
+      setError(errorMessage(cause));
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2" data-testid="context-agent-tools">
+      <Card title={`Agent Skills Library (${enabled.length}/${skills.length} enabled)`}>
+        <div className="space-y-2 px-5 py-4">
+          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
+            {(["claude", "copilot", "opencode"] as const).map((target) => (
+              <button
+                key={target}
+                type="button"
+                data-export={target}
+                disabled={readOnly || pending === `export-${target}`}
+                onClick={() => void exportSkills(target)}
+                className={cn(cqaBtnSecondary, "h-8 px-2.5 text-[11.5px]")}
+              >
+                <CqaIcon name="download" className="text-[14px]" />
+                {target === "claude" ? ".claude/CLAUDE.md" : target === "copilot" ? ".github/copilot-instructions.md" : "opencode.json"}
+              </button>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+          {skills.map((skill) => {
+            const on = enabled.includes(skill.id);
+            return (
+              <div
+                key={skill.id}
+                data-skill={skill.id}
+                className={cn("rounded-lg border px-3 py-2", on ? "border-[#c7d2fe] bg-[#faf9ff]" : "border-[#eef0f3]")}
+              >
+                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
+                  <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">
+                    <CqaIcon name={skill.icon} className={cn("text-[15px]", on ? "text-[#4f46e5]" : "text-[#9ca3af]")} />
+                    {skill.title}
+                  </p>
+                  <button
+                    type="button"
+                    role="switch"
+                    aria-checked={on}
+                    aria-label={`${on ? "Disable" : "Enable"} ${skill.title}`}
+                    disabled={readOnly || pending === skill.id}
+                    onClick={() => void toggle(skill)}
+                    className={cn("relative h-5 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-full transition disabled:opacity-50", on ? "bg-[#5b4ee8]" : "bg-[#d1d5db]")}
+                  >
+                    <span className={cn("absolute top-0.5 size-4 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition", on ? "left-[18px]" : "left-0.5")} />
+                  </button>
+                </div>
+                <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] leading-4 text-[#6b7280]">{skill.description}</p>
+              </div>
+            );
+          })}
+          {exportResult && (
+            <div className="pt-1">
+              <p className="mb-1 font-mono text-[10.5px] text-[#6b7280]">{exportResult.path}</p>
+              <pre className="max-h-40 overflow-auto rounded-lg bg-[#10141c] p-2.5 text-[10.5px] leading-4 text-[#e2e8f0]">
+                <code>{exportResult.content}</code>
+              </pre>
+            </div>
+          )}
+        </div>
+      </Card>
+
+      <Card title="Context7 Live Docs">
+        <div className="space-y-2 px-5 py-4" data-testid="context7-resolver">
+          <p className="text-[12px] leading-5 text-[#6b7280]">
+            Resolve a task to versioned library rules. The resolver feeds test generation and auto-healing with the same
+            Playwright, Next.js, Tailwind, Prisma and TypeScript constraints.
+          </p>
+          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
+            <input
+              className="h-9 min-w-[220px] flex-1 rounded-lg border border-[#e5e7eb] px-3 text-[12.5px] outline-none focus:border-[#a9a1f7] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#e4e1fd]"
+              value={query}
+              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
+              aria-label="Context7 query"
+            />
+            <button type="button" className={cqaBtnPrimary} disabled={loading} onClick={() => void resolve()}>
+              <CqaIcon name="search" className="text-[15px]" />
+              {loading ? "Resolving…" : "Resolve"}
+            </button>
+          </div>
+          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
+            {["Playwright", "Next.js", "Tailwind CSS", "Prisma", "TypeScript"].map((name) => (
+              <button
+                key={name}
+                type="button"
+                className="rounded-full bg-[#f3f4f6] px-2.5 py-1 text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#4b5563] hover:bg-[#e9eaee]"
+                onClick={() => {
+                  const next = `${name} best practice for QASyn tests`;
+                  setQuery(next);
+                  void resolve(next);
+                }}
+              >
+                {name}
+              </button>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+          {error && <p role="alert" className="rounded border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-2.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] text-rose-700">{error}</p>}
+          {result && (
+            <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#eef0f3] bg-[#fafafa] p-2.5">
+              <p className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">
+                {result.library.name} <span className="font-mono text-[10.5px] font-normal text-[#6b7280]">v{result.library.version}</span>
+              </p>
+              <ul className="mt-1 list-disc space-y-1 pl-4 text-[11px] leading-4 text-[#4b5563]">
+                {result.rules.map((rule) => <li key={rule}>{rule}</li>)}
+              </ul>
+              <a href={result.library.docsUrl} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="mt-1.5 inline-block text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#5b4ee8] hover:underline">
+                {result.source}
+              </a>
+            </div>
+          )}
+        </div>
+      </Card>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+"use client";
+
+import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
+import { CqaIcon, cqaCard } from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import type { GraphifyKind, GraphifyMap, GraphifyNode } from "@/lib/graphify";
+import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+const KIND_META: Record<GraphifyKind, { label: string; icon: string; color: string; fill: string }> = {
+  url: { label: "Crawled URLs", icon: "travel_explore", color: "#0284c7", fill: "#e0f2fe" },
+  dom: { label: "DOM components", icon: "widgets", color: "#7c3aed", fill: "#f3e8ff" },
+  requirement: { label: "Requirements", icon: "description", color: "#d97706", fill: "#fef3c7" },
+  test: { label: "Test Cases", icon: "fact_check", color: "#059669", fill: "#d1fae5" },
+  pr: { label: "Pull Requests", icon: "merge", color: "#e11d48", fill: "#ffe4e6" },
+};
+
+const LAYERS: GraphifyKind[] = ["url", "requirement", "test", "dom", "pr"];
+const NODE_WIDTH = 196;
+const NODE_HEIGHT = 54;
+const COLUMN_X: Record<GraphifyKind, number> = { url: 108, requirement: 352, test: 596, dom: 840, pr: 1084 };
+
+function truncate(value: string, max = 30) {
+  return value.length > max ? `${value.slice(0, max - 1)}…` : value;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Interactive SVG rendering of the Graphify quality graph.
+ * Filter by node kind, zoom with the controls and click any node for details.
+ */
+export function GraphifyMapView({ data, compact = false }: { data: GraphifyMap; compact?: boolean }) {
+  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
+  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState<Record<GraphifyKind, boolean>>({
+    url: true,
+    requirement: true,
+    test: true,
+    dom: true,
+    pr: true,
+  });
+  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
+
+  const visibleNodes = useMemo(() => data.nodes.filter((node) => enabled[node.kind]), [data.nodes, enabled]);
+  const visibleIds = useMemo(() => new Set(visibleNodes.map((node) => node.id)), [visibleNodes]);
+  const visibleEdges = useMemo(
+    () => data.edges.filter((edge) => visibleIds.has(edge.source) && visibleIds.has(edge.target)),
+    [data.edges, visibleIds],
+  );
+
+  const { positions, height } = useMemo(() => {
+    const grouped: Record<GraphifyKind, GraphifyNode[]> = { url: [], dom: [], requirement: [], test: [], pr: [] };
+    for (const node of visibleNodes) grouped[node.kind].push(node);
+    const rows = Math.max(1, ...LAYERS.map((kind) => grouped[kind].length));
+    const computedHeight = 84 + rows * 78;
+    const map = new Map<string, { x: number; y: number }>();
+    for (const kind of LAYERS) {
+      grouped[kind].forEach((node, index) => {
+        map.set(node.id, { x: COLUMN_X[kind], y: 70 + index * 78 });
+      });
+    }
+    return { positions: map, height: computedHeight };
+  }, [visibleNodes]);
+
+  const selectedNode = data.nodes.find((node) => node.id === selected) ?? null;
+  const selectedEdges = selectedNode
+    ? data.edges.filter((edge) => edge.source === selectedNode.id || edge.target === selectedNode.id)
+    : [];
+  const width = 1200;
+
+  function toggleKind(kind: GraphifyKind) {
+    setEnabled((prev) => ({ ...prev, [kind]: !prev[kind] }));
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <section className={cn(cqaCard, "overflow-hidden")} data-testid="graphify-map">
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#f0f1f3] px-4 py-3">
+        <div>
+          <h3 className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[14px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">
+            <CqaIcon name="hub" className="text-[18px] text-[#5b4ee8]" /> Graphify Map
+          </h3>
+          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11.5px] text-[#6b7280]">
+            {data.nodes.length} nodes · {data.edges.length} edges · crawled URLs, DOM, requirements, tests and PRs
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            data-testid="graphify-zoom-out"
+            aria-label="Zoom out"
+            className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white text-[#4b5563] hover:bg-[#f9fafb]"
+            onClick={() => setZoom((value) => Math.max(0.5, Number((value - 0.15).toFixed(2))))}
+          >
+            <CqaIcon name="remove" className="text-[17px]" />
+          </button>
+          <span className="w-11 text-center font-mono text-[11px] text-[#6b7280]">{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            data-testid="graphify-zoom-in"
+            aria-label="Zoom in"
+            className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white text-[#4b5563] hover:bg-[#f9fafb]"
+            onClick={() => setZoom((value) => Math.min(2, Number((value + 0.15).toFixed(2))))}
+          >
+            <CqaIcon name="add" className="text-[17px]" />
+          </button>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            data-testid="graphify-zoom-reset"
+            className="ml-1 rounded-md border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-[#4b5563] hover:bg-[#f9fafb]"
+            onClick={() => setZoom(1)}
+          >
+            Reset
+          </button>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 border-b border-[#f0f1f3] px-4 py-2">
+        {LAYERS.map((kind) => {
+          const meta = KIND_META[kind];
+          return (
+            <button
+              key={kind}
+              type="button"
+              data-filter={kind}
+              aria-pressed={enabled[kind]}
+              onClick={() => toggleKind(kind)}
+              className={cn(
+                "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-medium transition",
+                enabled[kind] ? "border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white text-[#374151]" : "border-[#f3f4f6] bg-[#f9fafb] text-[#9ca3af] line-through",
+              )}
+            >
+              <span className="size-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: meta.color }} />
+              {meta.label} ({data.stats[kind]})
+            </button>
+          );
+        })}
+      </div>
+
+      {visibleNodes.length === 0 ? (
+        <div className="px-4 py-14 text-center">
+          <p className="text-[13px] font-medium text-[#374151]">No graph nodes for this filter</p>
+          <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af]">Run a crawl or create test cases to populate the map.</p>
+        </div>
+      ) : (
+        <div className={cn("overflow-auto bg-[#fcfcfd]", compact ? "max-h-[420px]" : "max-h-[640px]")}>
+          <svg
+            viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`}
+            width={width * zoom}
+            height={height * zoom}
+            role="img"
+            aria-label="Graphify map connecting crawled URLs, requirements, tests, DOM components and pull requests"
+          >
+            <defs>
+              <marker id="graphify-arrow" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="9" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">
+                <path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="#cbd5e1" />
+              </marker>
+            </defs>
+
+            {LAYERS.map((kind) => {
+              const meta = KIND_META[kind];
+              return (
+                <text key={kind} x={COLUMN_X[kind] - NODE_WIDTH / 2} y={24} className="fill-[#6b7280] text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide">
+                  {meta.label}
+                </text>
+              );
+            })}
+
+            {visibleEdges.map((edge) => {
+              const from = positions.get(edge.source);
+              const to = positions.get(edge.target);
+              if (!from || !to) return null;
+              const x1 = from.x + NODE_WIDTH / 2;
+              const y1 = from.y + NODE_HEIGHT / 2;
+              const x2 = to.x - NODE_WIDTH / 2;
+              const y2 = to.y + NODE_HEIGHT / 2;
+              const control = Math.max(40, Math.abs(x2 - x1) / 2);
+              return (
+                <path
+                  key={edge.id}
+                  d={`M ${x1} ${y1} C ${x1 + control} ${y1}, ${x2 - control} ${y2}, ${x2} ${y2}`}
+                  fill="none"
+                  stroke="#d7dce3"
+                  strokeWidth={1.2}
+                  markerEnd="url(#graphify-arrow)"
+                />
+              );
+            })}
+
+            {visibleNodes.map((node) => {
+              const position = positions.get(node.id);
+              if (!position) return null;
+              const meta = KIND_META[node.kind];
+              const active = selected === node.id;
+              return (
+                <g
+                  key={node.id}
+                  data-node-kind={node.kind}
+                  data-node-id={node.id}
+                  transform={`translate(${position.x - NODE_WIDTH / 2}, ${position.y})`}
+                  onClick={() => setSelected(active ? null : node.id)}
+                  className="cursor-pointer"
+                  role="button"
+                  tabIndex={0}
+                  aria-label={`${meta.label}: ${node.label}`}
+                  onKeyDown={(event) => {
+                    if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") setSelected(active ? null : node.id);
+                  }}
+                >
+                  <rect
+                    width={NODE_WIDTH}
+                    height={NODE_HEIGHT}
+                    rx={10}
+                    fill={active ? meta.fill : "#ffffff"}
+                    stroke={meta.color}
+                    strokeWidth={active ? 2 : 1}
+                  />
+                  <rect width={4} height={NODE_HEIGHT} rx={2} fill={meta.color} />
+                  <text x={12} y={21} className="fill-[#111827] text-[11.5px] font-semibold">
+                    {truncate(node.label, 32)}
+                  </text>
+                  <text x={12} y={38} className="fill-[#6b7280] text-[10px]">
+                    {truncate(node.detail, 36)}
+                  </text>
+                </g>
+              );
+            })}
+          </svg>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {selectedNode && (
+        <div className="border-t border-[#f0f1f3] bg-[#fafafa] px-4 py-3" data-testid="graphify-detail">
+          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
+            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">
+              <span className="size-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: KIND_META[selectedNode.kind].color }} />
+              {selectedNode.label}
+            </p>
+            <button type="button" className="text-[11px] text-[#6b7280] hover:text-[#111827]" onClick={() => setSelected(null)}>
+              Close
+            </button>
+          </div>
+          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11.5px] text-[#6b7280]">
+            {KIND_META[selectedNode.kind].label} · {selectedNode.detail}
+          </p>
+          <ul className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
+            {selectedEdges.slice(0, 8).map((edge) => {
+              const other = data.nodes.find((node) => node.id === (edge.source === selectedNode.id ? edge.target : edge.source));
+              if (!other) return null;
+              return (
+                <li key={edge.id} className="rounded-full border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] text-[#4b5563]">
+                  {edge.relation} · {truncate(other.label, 26)}
+                </li>
+              );
+            })}
+            {selectedEdges.length === 0 && <li className="text-[10.5px] text-[#9ca3af]">No connected edge.</li>}
+          </ul>
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </section>
+  );
+}

+"use client";
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
+import { CqaIcon, cqaBtnPrimary, cqaBtnSecondary } from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import type { LocatorCandidate, MagicLocatorResult } from "@/lib/magic-locator";
+import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+interface Props {
+  step: { action: string; target: string; value: string; selector: string };
+  readOnly: boolean;
+  onApply: (selector: string) => void;
+}
+
+const STRATEGY_STYLE: Record<LocatorCandidate["strategy"], string> = {
+  Role: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200",
+  "Test ID": "bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 border-sky-200",
+  Label: "bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200",
+  Text: "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200",
+  CSS: "bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200",
+};
+
+/** ✨ Magic UI Component & Smart Locator inspector for a Studio step. */
+export function MagicLocatorInspector({ step, readOnly, onApply }: Props) {
+  const [result, setResult] = useState<MagicLocatorResult | null>(null);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [applied, setApplied] = useState<{ strategy: string; selector: string } | null>(null);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let cancelled = false;
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError(null);
+    fetch("/api/dashboard/magic-locator", {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+      body: JSON.stringify(step),
+    })
+      .then(async (response) => {
+        const payload = (await response.json().catch(() => null)) as (MagicLocatorResult & { error?: string }) | null;
+        if (!response.ok || !payload) throw new Error(payload?.error ?? `Locator engine returned ${response.status}.`);
+        if (!cancelled) setResult(payload);
+      })
+      .catch((cause: unknown) => {
+        if (!cancelled) setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Could not analyse this locator.");
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
+      });
+    return () => {
+      cancelled = true;
+    };
+  }, [step.action, step.target, step.value, step.selector]);
+
+  return (
+    <section
+      data-testid="magic-locator"
+      aria-label="Magic UI Component and Smart Locator"
+      className="rounded-xl border border-[#ddd6fe] bg-gradient-to-br from-[#faf9ff] to-white p-3.5"
+    >
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
+        <div>
+          <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-[#4c3fd6]">
+            <span aria-hidden="true">✨</span> Magic UI Component &amp; Smart Locator
+          </p>
+          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-[#6b7280]">
+            {result ? `${result.component} · ${result.intent}` : "Scores accessible, stable locators for this step."}
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <span className="rounded-full border border-[#ddd6fe] bg-white px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#5b4ee8]">
+          Magic-MCP
+        </span>
+      </div>
+
+      {loading && (
+        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-[11.5px] text-[#6b7280]">
+          <span className="size-3.5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-[#8b80ff] border-t-transparent" />
+          Scoring selectors…
+        </div>
+      )}
+      {error && (
+        <p role="alert" className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-2.5 py-2 text-[11.5px] text-rose-700">
+          {error}
+        </p>
+      )}
+
+      {result && (
+        <div className="mt-3 space-y-1.5">
+          {result.candidates.map((candidate, index) => (
+            <div
+              key={`${candidate.strategy}-${candidate.selector}`}
+              data-locator-strategy={candidate.strategy}
+              className={cn(
+                "rounded-lg border bg-white px-2.5 py-2",
+                index === 0 ? "border-[#c7d2fe] shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(79,70,229,0.08)]" : "border-[#eef0f3]",
+              )}
+            >
+              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+                <span className={cn("rounded border px-1.5 py-[1px] text-[10px] font-semibold", STRATEGY_STYLE[candidate.strategy])}>
+                  {candidate.strategy}
+                </span>
+                <code className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono text-[10.5px] text-[#374151]" title={candidate.selector}>
+                  {candidate.selector}
+                </code>
+                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
+                  <span className="h-1.5 w-14 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#eef0f3]">
+                    <span
+                      className={cn("block h-full rounded-full", candidate.score >= 90 ? "bg-emerald-500" : candidate.score >= 75 ? "bg-indigo-500" : "bg-amber-500")}
+                      style={{ width: `${candidate.score}%` }}
+                    />
+                  </span>
+                  <span className="w-8 text-right text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-[#374151]">{candidate.score}</span>
+                </span>
+              </div>
+              <p className="mt-1 text-[10.5px] leading-4 text-[#6b7280]">{candidate.reason}</p>
+            </div>
+          ))}
+
+          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
+            <p className="text-[10px] text-[#9ca3af]">
+              {applied?.selector === step.selector ? `Applied ${applied.strategy}` : result.best ? `Best score ${result.best.score}/100` : "No candidate"}
+            </p>
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              data-testid="magic-apply"
+              disabled={readOnly || !result.best}
+              onClick={() => {
+                if (!result.best) return;
+                onApply(result.best.selector);
+                setApplied({ strategy: result.best.strategy, selector: result.best.selector });
+              }}
+              className={cn(cqaBtnPrimary, "h-7 px-2.5 text-[11.5px]")}
+            >
+              <CqaIcon name="auto_fix_high" className="text-[14px]" /> Apply Best Locator
+            </button>
+          </div>
+          <details className="pt-1">
+            <summary className="cursor-pointer text-[10.5px] font-medium text-[#6b7280]">Magic guidance</summary>
+            <ul className="mt-1 list-disc space-y-0.5 pl-4 text-[10.5px] leading-4 text-[#6b7280]">
+              {result.guidance.map((line) => <li key={line}>{line}</li>)}
+            </ul>
+          </details>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {!loading && !result && !error && (
+        <button type="button" className={cn(cqaBtnSecondary, "mt-3 h-7 px-2.5 text-[11.5px]")} onClick={() => setLoading(true)}>
+          Retry analysis
+        </button>
+      )}
+    </section>
+  );
+}

+      setSkills({ ...skills, enabled: result.enabled });
+      toast(`${skill.title} ${enabled ? "disabled" : "enabled"}.`, "success");
+    } catch (error) {
+      toast(errorMessage(error), "error");
+    } finally {
+      setSkillPending(null);
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function exportSkills(target: "claude" | "copilot" | "opencode") {
+    setSkillPending(`export-${target}`);
+    try {
+      const result = await apiFetch<{ filename: string; path: string; content: string }>("/api/dashboard/agent-skills", {
+        method: "POST",
+        json: { action: "export", target },
+      });
+      setExportResult(result);
+      toast(`${result.path} generated. Copy or download it.`, "success");
+    } catch (error) {
+      toast(errorMessage(error), "error");
+    } finally {
+      setSkillPending(null);
+    }
+  }
+
+  function downloadExport() {
+    if (!exportResult) return;
+    const blob = new Blob([exportResult.content], { type: "text/plain;charset=utf-8" });
+    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
+    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
+    anchor.href = url;
+    anchor.download = exportResult.filename;
+    anchor.click();
+    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
+  }
+

+        score: number;
+        rules: string[];
+        injectedPrompt: string;
+        source: string;
+      }>("/api/dashboard/context7", { method: "POST", json: { query } });
+      setContextResult(result);
+    } catch (error) {
+      setContextError(errorMessage(error));
+    } finally {
+      setContextLoading(false);
+    }
+  }
