+"use client";
+
+import { useState } from "react";
+import { CqaIcon, cqaBtnPrimary, cqaCard, cqaInput } from "@/components/dashboard/ui";
+import { apiFetch, errorMessage } from "@/lib/api";
+import type { StrixFinding, StrixReport, StrixSeverity } from "@/lib/strix";
+import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
+
+const SEVERITY_STYLE: Record<StrixSeverity, string> = {
+  critical: "bg-[#fef2f2] text-[#b42318] border-[#fecdca]",
+  high: "bg-[#fff4ed] text-[#b93815] border-[#fed7aa]",
+  medium: "bg-[#fffaeb] text-[#b54708] border-[#fde68a]",
+  low: "bg-[#eff8ff] text-[#175cd3] border-[#bfdbfe]",
+  info: "bg-[#f9fafb] text-[#4b5563] border-[#e5e7eb]",
+};
+
+function GradeBadge({ grade, score }: { grade: StrixReport["grade"]; score: number }) {
+  const tone =
+    grade === "A" || grade === "B"
+      ? "border-[#abefc6] bg-[#ecfdf3] text-[#067647]"
+      : grade === "C"
+        ? "border-[#fde68a] bg-[#fffbeb] text-[#b54708]"
+        : "border-[#fecdca] bg-[#fef3f2] text-[#b42318]";
+  return (
+    <div className={cn("flex size-14 shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border", tone)} data-grade={grade}>
+      <span className="text-[20px] font-bold leading-5">{grade}</span>
+      <span className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium">{score}/100</span>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+function FindingRow({ finding }: { finding: StrixFinding }) {
+  return (
+    <li
+      data-finding={finding.id}
+      data-severity={finding.severity}
+      className="rounded-lg border border-[#eef0f3] px-3 py-2"
+    >
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
+        <span className={cn("rounded border px-1.5 py-[1px] text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase", SEVERITY_STYLE[finding.severity])}>
+          {finding.severity}
+        </span>
+        <span className="text-[12px] font-medium text-[#111827]">{finding.title}</span>
+        <span
+          className={cn(
+            "ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-1.5 py-[1px] text-[10px] font-semibold",
+            finding.passed ? "bg-[#ecfdf3] text-[#067647]" : "bg-[#fef3f2] text-[#b42318]",
+          )}
+        >
+          <CqaIcon name={finding.passed ? "check" : "close"} className="text-[12px]" />
+          {finding.passed ? "Pass" : "Fix"}
+        </span>
+      </div>
+      <p className="mt-1 break-words font-mono text-[10.5px] leading-4 text-[#6b7280]">{finding.evidence}</p>
+      {!finding.passed && (
+        <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] leading-4 text-[#374151]">
+          <span className="font-semibold">Remediation:</span> {finding.remediation}
+        </p>
+      )}
+      <p className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-[#9ca3af]">{finding.owasp}</p>
+    </li>
+  );
+}
+
+/** Passive Strix security scanner shared by Security testing and Red team. */
+export function StrixScanner({ readOnly = false, defaultTarget = "https://www.cgi.com" }: { readOnly?: boolean; defaultTarget?: string }) {
+  const [target, setTarget] = useState(defaultTarget);
+  const [report, setReport] = useState<StrixReport | null>(null);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
+
+  async function scan() {
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError(null);
+    try {
+      setReport(await apiFetch<StrixReport>("/api/dashboard/strix", { method: "POST", json: { url: target } }));
+    } catch (cause) {
+      setError(errorMessage(cause));
+      setReport(null);
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  const failed = report?.findings.filter((finding) => !finding.passed && finding.severity !== "info") ?? [];
+
+  return (
+    <section className={cn(cqaCard, "p-4")} data-testid="strix-scanner">
+      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">
+        <div>
+          <h2 className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[15px] font-semibold text-[#111827]">
+            <CqaIcon name="shield" className="text-[18px] text-[#4f46e5]" /> Strix AI Security &amp; OWASP Inspector
+          </h2>
+          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12px] text-[#6b7280]">
+            Passive audit of CSP, HSTS, frame protection, content-type, referrer, permissions, cookies and exposed
+            surface. One GET request, no exploit payload.
+          </p>
+        </div>

+type StepTone = { icon: string; tone: string; label: string };
+
+/** Icon + colour per action type, matching the ContextQA step list. */
+function stepVisual(step: StudioStep): StepTone {
+  const target = `${step.target} ${step.value}`.toLowerCase();
+  if (step.action === "AI Assertion") return { icon: "auto_awesome", tone: "bg-purple-50 text-purple-600", label: "AI Verification" };
+  if (step.action === "Navigate" || /url|navigate|page url/.test(target)) return { icon: "link", tone: "bg-rose-50 text-rose-500", label: "Navigate" };
+  if (step.action === "Enter Text" || /enter|type|fill|password/.test(target)) return { icon: "keyboard", tone: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600", label: "Enter" };
+  if (step.action === "Click") return { icon: "ads_click", tone: "bg-sky-50 text-sky-600", label: "Click" };
+  if (step.action === "Verify Element") return { icon: "fact_check", tone: "bg-sky-50 text-sky-600", label: "Verify" };
+  return { icon: "bolt", tone: "bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600", label: step.action };
+}
+
+/** Splits a generated sentence into an action prefix and the highlighted target/assertion. */
+function stepParts(step: StudioStep, variables: Record<string, string>, orgUrl: string) {
+  const sentence = sentenceForStep(step, variables, orgUrl);
+  switch (step.action) {
+    case "Navigate":
+      return { prefix: "Navigate to", focus: sentence.replace(/^Navigate to\s*/i, "") || orgUrl };
+    case "Click":
+      return { prefix: "Click on", focus: sentence.replace(/^Click on\s*/i, "") || "the target element" };
+    case "Enter Text": {
+      const match = /^Enter\s+(.+?)\s+in\s+(.+)$/i.exec(sentence);
+      return match
+        ? { prefix: "Enter", focus: `${match[1]} in ${match[2]}` }
+        : { prefix: "Enter text", focus: sentence };
+    }
+    case "AI Assertion":
+      return { prefix: "AI Verification", focus: sentence.replace(/^Verify that\s*/i, "") || sentence };
+    case "Verify Element":
+      return { prefix: "Verify that", focus: sentence.replace(/^Verify that\s*/i, "") || "the expected state is visible" };
+    default:
+      return { prefix: stepVisual(step).label, focus: sentence };
+  }
+}
+
+function formatRunClock(iso: string) {
+  return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString("en-US", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit", hour12: true });
+}
+
+function formatRunDay(iso: string) {
+  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "short", day: "numeric" });

+  async function persistSteps(next: StudioStep[], key = "save", recordHistory = true) {

+  function undoSteps() {
+    if (undoStack.length === 0) return;
+    const previous = undoStack[undoStack.length - 1];
+    setUndoStack((prev) => prev.slice(0, -1));
+    setRedoStack((prev) => [...prev, steps]);
+    setSteps(previous);
+    setSelectedSteps([]);
+    void run("undo", `/api/dashboard/test-cases/${data.id}`, { method: "PATCH", json: { action: "steps", steps: previous } });
+  }
+
+  function redoSteps() {
+    if (redoStack.length === 0) return;
+    const next = redoStack[redoStack.length - 1];
+    setRedoStack((prev) => prev.slice(0, -1));
+    setUndoStack((prev) => [...prev, steps]);
+    setSteps(next);
+    setSelectedSteps([]);
+    void run("redo", `/api/dashboard/test-cases/${data.id}`, { method: "PATCH", json: { action: "steps", steps: next } });
+  }
+
+  function toggleStepSelection(id: string) {
+    setSelectedSteps((prev) => (prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((entry) => entry !== id) : [...prev, id]));
+  }
+
+  function selectedIndexes() {
+    return steps.map((step, index) => (selectedSteps.includes(step.id) ? index : -1)).filter((index) => index >= 0);
+  }
+
+  function deleteSelected() {
+    const indexes = new Set(selectedIndexes());
+    if (indexes.size === 0) return;
+    setSelectedSteps([]);
+    setDetailIndex(null);
+    void persistSteps(steps.filter((_, index) => !indexes.has(index)), "bulk-delete");
+  }
+
+  function duplicateSelected() {
+    const indexes = new Set(selectedIndexes());
+    if (indexes.size === 0) return;
+    const clones = steps
+      .filter((_, index) => indexes.has(index))
+      .map((step) => ({ ...step, id: `s${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}` }));
+    setSelectedSteps([]);
+    void persistSteps([...steps, ...clones], "bulk-duplicate");
+  }
+
+  function toggleDisabledSelected() {
+    const indexes = new Set(selectedIndexes());
+    if (indexes.size === 0) return;
+    const allDisabled = steps.every((step, index) => !indexes.has(index) || step.disabled);
+    void persistSteps(
+      steps.map((step, index) => (indexes.has(index) ? { ...step, disabled: !allDisabled } : step)),
+      "bulk-disable",
+    );
+  }
+
+  function moveSelected(direction: -1 | 1) {
+    const indexes = selectedIndexes();
+    if (indexes.length === 0) return;
+    const next = [...steps];
+    const ordered = direction < 0 ? indexes : [...indexes].reverse();
+    let moved = false;
+    for (const index of ordered) {
+      const target = index + direction;
+      if (target < 0 || target >= next.length) continue;
+      if (selectedSteps.includes(next[target].id)) continue;
+      [next[index], next[target]] = [next[target], next[index]];
+      moved = true;
+    }
+    if (moved) void persistSteps(next, "bulk-move");
+  }

+/**
+ * Agent Skills Library — six QA engineering skills inspired by
+ * addyosmani/agent-skills. They are plain Markdown instructions that can be
+ * enabled per organisation and exported to Claude Code, GitHub Copilot and
+ * OpenCode without any runtime dependency.
+ */
+
+export interface AgentSkill {
+  id: string;
+  name: string;
+  title: string;
+  description: string;
+  category: "Testing" | "Review" | "Accessibility" | "Security" | "Reliability";
+  icon: string;
+  triggers: string[];
+  tools: string[];
+  body: string;
+}
+
+export const AGENT_SKILLS: AgentSkill[] = [
+  {
+    id: "playwright-self-healing",
+    name: "playwright-self-healing",
+    title: "Playwright Self-Healing",
+    description: "Repairs broken locators with accessible, resilient selectors and re-runs the failed step.",
+    category: "Testing",
+    icon: "auto_fix_high",
+    triggers: ["selector changed", "element not found", "auto-heal", "flaky locator"],
+    tools: ["qasyn_run_test_case", "qasyn_get_execution_evidence", "qasyn_context7_resolve"],
+    body: `## playwright-self-healing
+When a Playwright step fails because a locator no longer matches:
+1. Fetch the execution evidence and the failing step selector.
+2. Resolve Context7 Playwright rules before proposing a replacement.
+3. Rank replacements: getByRole > getByLabel > getByTestId > getByText > CSS.
+4. Never use nth-child, generated utility classes or absolute XPath.
+5. Apply the best locator, re-run only the failed step, and keep the old selector as fallback.
+6. Report the before/after selector and the confidence score.`,
+  },
+  {
+    id: "pr-blast-radius-gate",
+    name: "pr-blast-radius-gate",
+    title: "PR Blast Radius Gate",
+    description: "Maps changed files to impacted tests and blocks merges when critical coverage is at risk.",
+    category: "Review",
+    icon: "merge",
+    triggers: ["pull request", "PR review", "impact analysis", "merge gate"],
+    tools: ["qasyn_analyze_pr", "qasyn_list_test_cases", "qasyn_run_test_plan"],
+    body: `## pr-blast-radius-gate
+For every pull request:
+1. Collect changed files, owned modules and touched routes.
+2. Map modules to test cases; mark critical/high tests as mandatory.
+3. Compute blast radius: low (<8 files), medium (8-24), high (25+).
+4. Run impacted tests. A critical failure means No-Go.
+5. Post a verdict with evidence: files, tests run, regressions, uncovered paths.
+6. Never approve solely from a green build when impacted critical tests were skipped.`,
+  },
+  {
+    id: "tdd-spec-generator",
+    name: "tdd-spec-generator",
+    title: "TDD Spec Generator",
+    description: "Turns a requirement into executable Given/When/Then specs before implementation.",
+    category: "Testing",
+    icon: "fact_check",
+    triggers: ["new requirement", "acceptance criteria", "spec", "TDD", "test first"],
+    tools: ["qasyn_create_web_test", "qasyn_list_test_cases"],
+    body: `## tdd-spec-generator
+1. Read the requirement and extract actors, preconditions, actions and outcomes.
+2. Write Given/When/Then scenarios for happy path, negative path, boundary and permission cases.
+3. Give every scenario a stable title and trace it to the requirement id.
+4. Create the test cases before implementation, with concrete data and observable assertions.
+5. Reject vague assertions ("page works"); assert URL, role, text, state or persisted data.`,
+  },
+  {
+    id: "wcag-a11y-auditor",
+    name: "wcag-a11y-auditor",
+    title: "WCAG Accessibility Auditor",
+    description: "Audits pages against WCAG 2.2 AA and opens actionable defects.",

+export const DEFAULT_ENABLED_SKILLS = ["playwright-self-healing", "pr-blast-radius-gate", "tdd-spec-generator"];
+
+export function normalizeEnabledSkills(value: unknown): string[] {
+  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [...DEFAULT_ENABLED_SKILLS];
+  const known = new Set(AGENT_SKILLS.map((skill) => skill.id));
+  return value.filter((entry): entry is string => typeof entry === "string" && known.has(entry));
+}
+
+export function enabledSkillObjects(ids: string[]): AgentSkill[] {
+  return AGENT_SKILLS.filter((skill) => ids.includes(skill.id));
+}
+
+function header(target: "claude" | "copilot"): string {
+  return target === "claude"
+    ? "# QASyn Agent Skills\n\nGenerated by the QASyn Agent Skills Library. Enable/disable skills in Dashboard → MCP or Dashboard → Context.\n"
+    : "# QASyn agent instructions\n\nGenerated by the QASyn Agent Skills Library. Apply these rules to every QA, review and security task in this repository.\n";
+}
+
+export function buildClaudeInstructions(ids: string[]): string {
+  const skills = enabledSkillObjects(ids);
+  return `${header("claude")}\n${skills.map((skill) => `# ${skill.title}\n\n${skill.body}\n`).join("\n")}`;
+}
+
+export function buildCopilotInstructions(ids: string[]): string {
+  const skills = enabledSkillObjects(ids);
+  return `${header("copilot")}\n${skills.map((skill) => `## ${skill.title}\n\n${skill.body}\n`).join("\n")}`;
+}
+
+/** Valid opencode.json with the local QASyn MCP server and the enabled skills. */
+export function buildOpenCodeConfig(ids: string[]): string {
+  const skills = enabledSkillObjects(ids);
+  return JSON.stringify(

+      mcp: {
+        qasyn: {

+          url: "http://localhost:3000/api/mcp/sse",

+          description: "QA engineer using QASyn MCP tools and the enabled Agent Skills.",
+          mode: "primary",
+          prompt: skills.map((skill) => `# ${skill.title}\n${skill.body}`).join("\n\n") || "Use QASyn MCP tools to test this workspace.",
+        },
+      },
+    },
+    null,
+    2,
+  );

+/**
+ * Context7 Live Docs resolver.
+ *
+ * Mirrors the upstash/context7 workflow: resolve a natural-language QA task to
+ * a versioned library, return the current rules that matter for test
+ * generation/healing, and inject them into an agent prompt. The catalogue is
+ * local and deterministic so generation never depends on an external outage.
+ */
+
+export interface Context7Library {
+  id: string;
+  name: string;
+  version: string;
+  docsUrl: string;
+  keywords: string[];
+  rules: string[];
+  snippet: string;
+}
+
+export interface Context7Resolution {
+  resolved: boolean;
+  score: number;
+  library: Context7Library;
+  query: string;
+  rules: string[];
+  injectedPrompt: string;
+  source: string;
+}
+
+export const CONTEXT7_LIBRARIES: Context7Library[] = [
+  {
+    id: "playwright",
+    name: "Playwright",
+    version: "1.65",
+    docsUrl: "https://playwright.dev/docs/intro",
+    keywords: ["playwright", "browser", "selector", "locator", "click", "navigate", "test", "e2e", "page"],
+    rules: [
+      "Use user-facing locators first: getByRole, getByLabel, getByPlaceholder, getByText; use getByTestId only when there is no accessible name.",
+      "Never use nth-child or long CSS chains as the primary locator.",
+      "Use expect(locator).toBeVisible() with auto-waiting instead of fixed sleeps; set explicit timeouts only for known slow transitions.",
+      "Prefer web-first assertions and avoid waitForTimeout in committed tests.",
+      "Isolate state per test (storageState, fixtures) and capture trace/screenshot/video on failure.",
+    ],
+    snippet: "page.getByRole('button', { name: /sign in/i }); await expect(page.getByRole('heading')).toBeVisible();",
+  },
+  {
+    id: "nextjs",
+    name: "Next.js",
+    version: "15.5",
+    docsUrl: "https://nextjs.org/docs",
+    keywords: ["next", "nextjs", "app router", "server component", "route handler", "hydration", "server action"],
+    rules: [
+      "Wait for hydration/network idle before asserting interactive state in App Router pages.",
+      "Server Components cannot use browser APIs; exercise them through user-visible UI or Route Handlers.",
+      "Use data-testid sparingly; prefer semantic roles rendered by Server Components.",
+      "Route Handlers are HTTP endpoints: test status, JSON schema and auth, not React internals.",
+    ],
+    snippet: "await page.goto('/dashboard'); await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: /dashboard/i })).toBeVisible();",
+  },
+  {
+    id: "tailwind",
+    name: "Tailwind CSS",
+    version: "4.1",
+    docsUrl: "https://tailwindcss.com/docs",
+    keywords: ["tailwind", "css", "class", "responsive", "viewport", "layout", "style"],
+    rules: [
+      "Never select by generated utility classes; they change with design refactors.",
+      "Test responsive breakpoints with explicit viewport sizes and assert visible labels, not class names.",
+      "Use role/text assertions for hover/focus states; CSS class assertions are not behavioural.",
+    ],
+    snippet: "await page.setViewportSize({ width: 375, height: 812 }); await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /menu/i })).toBeVisible();",
+  },
+  {
+    id: "prisma",
+    name: "Prisma",
+    version: "6.16",
+    docsUrl: "https://www.prisma.io/docs",
+    keywords: ["prisma", "database", "sqlite", "postgres", "orm", "migration", "seed", "query"],
+    rules: [
+      "Assert persisted state through the API/database, never only through optimistic UI.",
+      "Scope every query by organisationId in multi-tenant flows.",
+      "Use transactions for multi-row invariants; clean up test fixtures by id.",
+    ],
+    snippet: "const record = await prisma.testCase.findFirst({ where: { id, organisationId } }); expect(record).not.toBeNull();",
+  },
+  {
+    id: "typescript",
+    name: "TypeScript",
+    version: "5.6",
+    docsUrl: "https://www.typescriptlang.org/docs",
+    keywords: ["typescript", "types", "type", "tsc", "compile", "interface", "generic"],
+    rules: [
+      "Keep external payloads unknown until validated (zod/type guard); never cast blindly.",
+      "Prefer discriminated unions for step/action variants so exhaustive UI rendering is checked.",
+      "Run tsc --noEmit in CI; test code is type-checked too.",
+    ],
+    snippet: "type StepAction = 'Navigate' | 'Click' | 'Enter Text'; function render(action: StepAction) { switch (action) { /* exhaustive */ } }",
+  },
+];
+
+function scoreLibrary(library: Context7Library, query: string): number {
+  const needle = query.toLowerCase();
+  let score = 0;
+  for (const keyword of library.keywords) {
+    if (needle.includes(keyword)) score += keyword.length > 4 ? 14 : 8;
+  }
+  if (needle.includes(library.name.toLowerCase())) score += 30;
+  return score;
+}
+
+/** Resolve the most relevant library and return prompt-ready rules. */
+export function resolveContext7(query: string): Context7Resolution {
+  const trimmed = query.trim();
+  const ranked = CONTEXT7_LIBRARIES.map((library) => ({ library, score: scoreLibrary(library, trimmed) })).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
+  const best = ranked[0] ?? { library: CONTEXT7_LIBRARIES[0], score: 0 };
+  const resolved = best.score > 0;
+  const rules = best.library.rules;
+  const injectedPrompt = [
+    `Context7 live documentation · ${best.library.name} v${best.library.version}`,
+    `Source: ${best.library.docsUrl}`,
+    ...rules.map((rule) => `- ${rule}`),
+    `Reference snippet: ${best.library.snippet}`,
+  ].join("\n");
+  return {
+    resolved,
+    score: best.score,
+    library: best.library,
+    query: trimmed,
+    rules,
+    injectedPrompt,
+    source: best.library.docsUrl,
+  };
+}
+
+/** Short guidance block injected into generation and self-healing prompts. */
+export function context7Guidance(query: string, limit = 3): string {
+  const resolution = resolveContext7(query);
+  return resolution.rules.slice(0, limit).map((rule) => `Context7/${resolution.library.name}: ${rule}`).join("\n");
+}

diff --git a/lib/graphify.ts b/lib/graphify.ts

+/**
+ * Graphify Map — a single connected view of the quality graph.
+ *
+ * Crawled URLs, DOM components discovered in test steps, Requirements,
+ * Test Cases and Pull Requests are projected into a deterministic layered
+ * graph so the UI can render (and filter/zoom) an interactive SVG.
+ */
+import { prisma } from "./db";
+
+export type GraphifyKind = "url" | "dom" | "requirement" | "test" | "pr";
+
+export interface GraphifyNode {
+  id: string;
+  label: string;
+  kind: GraphifyKind;
+  detail: string;
+  meta: Record<string, string | number>;
+}
+
+export interface GraphifyEdge {
+  id: string;
+  source: string;
+  target: string;
+  relation: string;
+}
+
+export interface GraphifyMap {
+  nodes: GraphifyNode[];
+  edges: GraphifyEdge[];
+  stats: Record<GraphifyKind, number>;
+  generatedAt: string;
+  focusCrawlId?: string;
+}
+
+export interface GraphifyOptions {
+  crawlId?: string;
+  limit?: number;
+}
+
+function parseJson<T>(value: string | null | undefined, fallback: T): T {
+  try {
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(value ?? "");
+    return (parsed ?? fallback) as T;
+  } catch {
+    return fallback;
+  }
+}
+
+function short(value: string, max = 54): string {
+  const trimmed = value.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
+  return trimmed.length > max ? `${trimmed.slice(0, max - 1)}…` : trimmed;
+}
+
+function domKey(action: string, step: { target?: string; selector?: string }): string {
+  const raw = (step.selector || step.target || action).toLowerCase();
+  return `${action}:${raw}`.replace(/[^a-z0-9:_-]+/g, "-").slice(0, 90);
+}
+
+function domComponent(action: string, target: string): string {
+  const lower = `${action} ${target}`.toLowerCase();
+  if (/link|nav/.test(lower)) return "Link";
+  if (/button|submit|click/.test(lower)) return "Button";
+  if (/enter|input|field|search|password|email/.test(lower)) return "TextField";
+  if (/check|radio|toggle/.test(lower)) return "Checkbox";
+  if (/select|dropdown/.test(lower)) return "Select";
+  if (/heading|title/.test(lower)) return "Heading";
+  if (/image|logo|icon/.test(lower)) return "Image";
+  if (/url|navigate|page/.test(lower)) return "Page";
+  return "Element";
+}
+
+/** Builds the organisation graph. Every query is scoped to one organisation. */
+export async function loadGraphifyMap(organisationId: string, options: GraphifyOptions = {}): Promise<GraphifyMap> {
+  const take = Math.max(4, Math.min(options.limit ?? 24, 60));
+  const [crawls, requirements, testCases, pullRequests] = await Promise.all([
+    prisma.crawlSession.findMany({
+      where: { organisationId, ...(options.crawlId ? { id: options.crawlId } : {}) },
+      orderBy: { startedAt: "desc" },
+      take: 8,
+      select: { id: true, targetUrl: true, status: true, flowsMapped: true, pagesVisited: true, requirementId: true },
+    }),
+    prisma.requirement.findMany({
+      where: { organisationId },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
+      take: 10,
+      include: { testCases: { select: { id: true }, take: 20 } },
+    }),
+    prisma.testCase.findMany({
+      where: { organisationId },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
+      take,
+      select: { id: true, title: true, module: true, status: true, stepsJson: true },
+    }),
+    prisma.pullRequestCheck.findMany({
+      where: { organisationId },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
+      take: 8,
+      select: { id: true, prNumber: true, title: true, status: true, riskLevel: true, impactJson: true },
+    }),
+  ]);
+
+  const nodes: GraphifyNode[] = [];
+  const edges: GraphifyEdge[] = [];
+  const stats: Record<GraphifyKind, number> = { url: 0, dom: 0, requirement: 0, test: 0, pr: 0 };
+
+  const addNode = (node: GraphifyNode) => {
+    if (nodes.some((existing) => existing.id === node.id)) return;
+    nodes.push(node);
+    stats[node.kind] += 1;
+  };
+  const addEdge = (source: string, target: string, relation: string) => {
+    if (!nodes.some((node) => node.id === source) || !nodes.some((node) => node.id === target)) return;
+    if (edges.some((edge) => edge.source === source && edge.target === target)) return;
+    edges.push({ id: `e-${edges.length + 1}`, source, target, relation });
+  };
+
+  const testIds = new Set(testCases.map((test) => `test:${test.id}`));
+  const requirementIds = new Set(requirements.map((requirement) => `req:${requirement.id}`));
+
+  for (const crawl of crawls) {
+    const id = `url:${crawl.id}`;
+    addNode({
+      id,
+      kind: "url",
+      label: short(crawl.targetUrl, 46),
+      detail: `${crawl.status} · ${crawl.pagesVisited} pages · ${crawl.flowsMapped} flows`,
+      meta: { crawlId: crawl.id, flows: crawl.flowsMapped },
+    });
+    if (crawl.requirementId && requirementIds.has(`req:${crawl.requirementId}`)) {
+      addEdge(id, `req:${crawl.requirementId}`, "crawled into");
+    }
+  }
+
+  for (const requirement of requirements) {
+    const id = `req:${requirement.id}`;
+    addNode({
+      id,
+      kind: "requirement",
+      label: `${requirement.code} · ${short(requirement.title, 38)}`,
+      detail: `${requirement.coverageStatus} · ${requirement.linkedTestsCount} linked tests`,
+      meta: { code: requirement.code, coverage: requirement.coverageStatus },
+    });
+    for (const test of requirement.testCases) {
+      if (testIds.has(`test:${test.id}`)) addEdge(id, `test:${test.id}`, "covered by");
+    }
+  }
+
+  const domByKey = new Map<string, { label: string; component: string; testIds: string[] }>();
+  for (const test of testCases) {
+    addNode({
+      id: `test:${test.id}`,
+      kind: "test",
+      label: short(test.title, 42),
+      detail: `${test.module} · ${test.status}`,
+      meta: { module: test.module, status: test.status },
+    });
+    const steps = parseJson<Array<{ action?: string; target?: string; selector?: string }>>(test.stepsJson, []);
+    for (const step of steps.slice(0, 6)) {
+      const action = step.action ?? "Element";
+      if (action === "Navigate") continue;
+      const key = domKey(action, step);
+      const component = domComponent(action, step.target ?? "");
+      const existing = domByKey.get(key);
+      if (existing) {
+        if (!existing.testIds.includes(test.id)) existing.testIds.push(test.id);
+      } else {
+        domByKey.set(key, {
+          label: short(step.target || step.selector || action, 40),
+          component,
+          testIds: [test.id],
+        });
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  let domIndex = 0;
+  for (const [key, value] of domByKey) {
+    if (domIndex >= 16) break;
+    domIndex += 1;
+    const id = `dom:${key.slice(0, 60)}:${domIndex}`;
+    addNode({
+      id,
+      kind: "dom",
+      label: value.label,
+      detail: `${value.component} · used by ${value.testIds.length} test(s)`,
+      meta: { component: value.component, tests: value.testIds.length },
+    });
+    for (const testId of value.testIds.slice(0, 3)) addEdge(`test:${testId}`, id, "asserts");
+  }
+
+  for (const pr of pullRequests) {
+    const id = `pr:${pr.id}`;
+    addNode({
+      id,
+      kind: "pr",
+      label: `#${pr.prNumber} ${short(pr.title, 38)}`,
+      detail: `${pr.status} · risk ${pr.riskLevel}`,
+      meta: { prNumber: pr.prNumber, risk: pr.riskLevel },
+    });
+    const impact = parseJson<{ scopeTestCaseIds?: string[] }>(pr.impactJson, {});
+    for (const testId of (impact.scopeTestCaseIds ?? []).slice(0, 6)) {
+      if (testIds.has(`test:${testId}`)) addEdge(id, `test:${testId}`, "impacts");
+    }
+  }
+
+  // A crawl with no requirement still anchors its DOM nodes to the URL so the
+  // graph is always connected when tests exist.
+  const firstUrl = nodes.find((node) => node.kind === "url");
+  if (firstUrl) {
+    for (const dom of nodes.filter((node) => node.kind === "dom").slice(0, 6)) {
+      addEdge(firstUrl.id, dom.id, "discovers");
+    }
+  }
+
+  const order: Record<GraphifyKind, number> = { url: 0, requirement: 1, test: 2, dom: 3, pr: 4 };
+  nodes.sort((a, b) => order[a.kind] - order[b.kind] || a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
+
+  return {
+    nodes,
+    edges,
+    stats,
+    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    focusCrawlId: options.crawlId,
+  };
+}
diff --git a/lib/healing.ts b/lib/healing.ts
index 917b9b7..e985e6c 100644
--- a/lib/healing.ts
+++ b/lib/healing.ts
@@ -2,7 +2,11 @@
  * DOM similarity auto-healing: when a recorded selector no longer matches,
  * score every candidate element of the current DOM against the originally
  * recorded element (tag, attributes, text, position) and pick the best one.
+ *
+ * Context7 Playwright rules are injected into every healing decision so the
+ * replacement prefers accessible, stable selectors over generated CSS chains.
  */
+import { context7Guidance } from "./context7";
 export interface DomElement {
   tag: string;
   attrs: Record<string, string>;
@@ -66,18 +70,29 @@ export interface HealResult {
   score: number;
   newSelector: string | null;
   candidate?: DomElement;
+  strategy?: "Test ID" | "Role" | "Text" | "CSS";
+  guidance: string;
 }
 
 /** Tries to heal a broken selector against the current DOM. */
 export function healSelector(recorded: RecordedElement, candidates: DomElement[], threshold = 0.45): HealResult {
+  const guidance = context7Guidance("playwright locator healing accessible role", 3);

   for (const el of candidates) {
     const score = similarity(recorded, el);
     if (!best || score > best.score) best = { el, score };
   }

+/**
+ * Magic UI — smart locator generator.
+ *
+ * Produces the same family of resilient Playwright locators that the
+ * 21st.dev magic-mcp workflow recommends, and scores them from most to least
+ * resilient: accessible role/name > test id > label > text > raw CSS.
+ */
+
+export interface LocatorStepInput {
+  action: string;
+  target: string;
+  value?: string;
+  selector?: string;
+}
+
+export interface LocatorCandidate {
+  strategy: "Role" | "Test ID" | "Label" | "Text" | "CSS";
+  selector: string;
+  score: number;
+  component: string;
+  reason: string;
+}
+
+export interface MagicLocatorResult {
+  component: string;
+  intent: string;
+  best: LocatorCandidate | null;
+  candidates: LocatorCandidate[];
+  guidance: string[];
+}
+
+function clean(value: string): string {
+  return value
+    .replace(/\*?\|[\w.-]+\|/g, " ")
+    .replace(/^["'`]+|["'`]+$/g, "")
+    .replace(/^(click on|click|navigate to|navigate|enter|type|fill|verify that|verify)\s+/i, "")
+    .replace(/\s+in\s+the\s+.+$/i, "")
+    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
+    .trim();
+}
+
+function slug(value: string): string {
+  return value
+    .toLowerCase()
+    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
+    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
+    .slice(0, 48) || "target";
+}
+
+function escapeRegExp(value: string): string {
+  return value.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
+}
+
+function isInput(action: string, target: string): boolean {
+  return /enter|type|fill|input|field|password|email|search/i.test(`${action} ${target}`);
+}
+
+function inferComponent(action: string, target: string): string {
+  const lower = `${action} ${target}`.toLowerCase();
+  if (/heading|title|h1|h2/.test(lower)) return "Heading";
+  if (/link|href|navigation/.test(lower)) return "Link";
+  if (/checkbox|radio|toggle/.test(lower)) return "Checkbox";
+  if (/select|dropdown|combobox/.test(lower)) return "Select";
+  if (isInput(action, target)) return "TextField";
+  if (/click|button|submit|sign in|login|continue|next/.test(lower)) return "Button";
+  if (/image|logo|icon/.test(lower)) return "Image";
+  return "Element";
+}
+
+function roleFor(component: string): string {
+  switch (component) {
+    case "Heading": return "heading";
+    case "Link": return "link";
+    case "Checkbox": return "checkbox";
+    case "Select": return "combobox";
+    case "TextField": return "textbox";
+    case "Button": return "button";
+    case "Image": return "img";
+    default: return "*";
+  }
+}
+
+export function generateMagicLocators(step: LocatorStepInput): MagicLocatorResult {
+  const label = clean(step.target) || clean(step.value ?? "") || "target";
+  const component = inferComponent(step.action, step.target || step.value || "");
+  const role = roleFor(component);
+  const safe = escapeRegExp(label);
+  const candidates: LocatorCandidate[] = [];
+
+  if (role !== "*") {
+    candidates.push({
+      strategy: "Role",
+      component,
+      selector: `page.getByRole('${role}', { name: /${safe}/i })`,
+      score: component === "TextField" ? 94 : 96,
+      reason: "Accessible role + name: resilient to DOM/CSS refactors and validates accessibility.",
+    });
+  }
+
+  candidates.push({
+    strategy: "Test ID",
+    component,
+    selector: `page.getByTestId('${slug(label)}')`,
+    score: 90,
+    reason: "data-testid is an explicit test contract and survives layout changes.",
+  });
+
+  if (isInput(step.action, step.target)) {
+    candidates.push({
+      strategy: "Label",
+      component,
+      selector: `page.getByLabel(/${safe}/i)`,
+      score: 88,
+      reason: "Label association is required for accessible forms and beats CSS for inputs.",
+    });
+  }
+
+  candidates.push({
+    strategy: "Text",
+    component,
+    selector: `page.getByText(/${safe}/i)`,
+    score: 76,
+    reason: "Visible text is user-facing but can change with copy edits or localisation.",
+  });
+
+  const existing = (step.selector || "").trim();
+  const cssSelector = existing.startsWith("css:")
+    ? existing.slice(4)
+    : existing || (component === "Button"
+      ? `button[type="submit"]`
+      : `text=${label}`);
+  candidates.push({
+    strategy: "CSS",
+    component,
+    selector: `page.locator(${JSON.stringify(cssSelector)})`,
+    score: existing ? 62 : 48,
+    reason: existing
+      ? "Existing CSS selector kept as fallback; prefer role/test-id when available."
+      : "Generated fallback CSS; brittle when markup or class names change.",
+  });
+
+  candidates.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
+  return {
+    component,
+    intent: `${step.action} ${label}`.trim(),
+    best: candidates[0] ?? null,
+    candidates,
+    guidance: [
+      "Prefer getByRole with an accessible name for interactive controls.",
+      "Add data-testid only where role/label cannot uniquely identify the element.",
+      "Keep CSS selectors as a last-resort fallback, never as the primary locator.",
+    ],
+  };
+}

+import type { Organisation } from "@prisma/client";
+import { decryptSecret, encryptSecret, generateApiToken, sha256 } from "@/lib/crypto";
+import { prisma } from "@/lib/db";
+import { readSettings, writeSettings } from "@/lib/tenant";
+
+export interface DefaultMcpToken {
+  token: string;
+  record: { id: string; name: string; tokenPrefix: string; scopesJson: string; createdAt: Date; lastUsedAt: Date | null };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Ensures the MCP page always has a real, selected cqa_ token.
+ *
+ * The secret is stored encrypted (AES-256-GCM via QASYN_SECRET) in the
+ * organisation settings so it can be re-used across page loads without ever
+ * storing plaintext. Revoking the token creates a fresh one on next visit.
+ */
+export async function ensureDefaultMcpToken(org: Pick<Organisation, "id" | "settingsJson">): Promise<DefaultMcpToken> {
+  const settings = readSettings(org);
+  const stored = settings.mcpDefaultToken;
+
+  if (stored?.tokenId && stored.secret) {
+    const token = decryptSecret(stored.secret);
+    if (token) {
+      const existing = await prisma.apiToken.findFirst({
+        where: { id: stored.tokenId, organisationId: org.id, tokenHash: sha256(token) },
+        select: { id: true, name: true, tokenPrefix: true, scopesJson: true, createdAt: true, lastUsedAt: true },
+      });
+      if (existing) return { token, record: existing };
+    }
+  }
+
+  const count = await prisma.apiToken.count({ where: { organisationId: org.id } });
+  if (count >= 10) {
+    const oldest = await prisma.apiToken.findFirst({ where: { organisationId: org.id }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" }, select: { id: true } });
+    if (oldest) await prisma.apiToken.delete({ where: { id: oldest.id } }).catch(() => undefined);
+  }
+  if (stored?.tokenId) {
+    await prisma.apiToken.deleteMany({ where: { id: stored.tokenId, organisationId: org.id } }).catch(() => undefined);
+  }
+
+  const { token, prefix, hash } = generateApiToken();
+  const record = await prisma.apiToken.create({
+    data: {
+      organisationId: org.id,
+      name: "MCP · default",
+      tokenPrefix: prefix,
+      tokenHash: hash,
+      scopesJson: JSON.stringify(["mcp:read", "mcp:run"]),
+    },
+    select: { id: true, name: true, tokenPrefix: true, scopesJson: true, createdAt: true, lastUsedAt: true },
+  });
+  await writeSettings(prisma, org, { mcpDefaultToken: { tokenId: record.id, secret: encryptSecret(token) } });
+  return { token, record };
+}

diff --git a/lib/strix.ts b/lib/strix.ts

index 0000000..d793434

@@ -0,0 +1,358 @@
+/**
+ * Strix Security — passive, defensive web surface scanner.
+ *
+ * It performs exactly one GET request (redirects are not followed) and inspects
+ * response headers, cookies and the first 200 KB of HTML. It never sends an
+ * exploit payload and refuses private/loopback targets (SSRF protection).
+ */
+
+export type StrixSeverity = "critical" | "high" | "medium" | "low" | "info";
+
+export interface StrixFinding {
+  id: string;
+  title: string;
+  severity: StrixSeverity;
+  category: "headers" | "cookies" | "exposure" | "transport";
+  passed: boolean;
+  evidence: string;
+  remediation: string;
+  owasp: string;
+}
+
+export interface StrixCookie {
+  name: string;
+  secure: boolean;
+  httpOnly: boolean;
+  sameSite: string;
+}
+
+export interface StrixReport {
+  target: string;
+  scannedAt: string;
+  reachable: boolean;
+  httpStatus: number | null;
+  score: number;
+  grade: "A" | "B" | "C" | "D" | "F";
+  findings: StrixFinding[];
+  headers: Record<string, string>;
+  cookies: StrixCookie[];
+  surface: string[];
+  error?: string;
+  notes: string[];
+}
+
+const PRIVATE_HOST = /^(localhost|127\.|0\.|10\.|169\.254\.|192\.168\.|172\.(1[6-9]|2\d|3[01])\.|\[?::1\]?|.*\.local$|.*\.internal$)/i;
+
+/** Only public http(s) targets may be scanned. */
+export function isPassiveScanTarget(value: string): boolean {
+  let url: URL;
+  try {
+    url = new URL(value);
+  } catch {
+    return false;
+  }
+  if (url.protocol !== "http:" && url.protocol !== "https:") return false;
+  if (url.username || url.password) return false;
+  if (PRIVATE_HOST.test(url.hostname)) return false;
+  return true;
+}
+
+function severityPenalty(severity: StrixSeverity): number {
+  switch (severity) {
+    case "critical": return 30;
+    case "high": return 18;
+    case "medium": return 8;
+    case "low": return 3;
+    default: return 0;
+  }
+}
+
+function gradeFor(score: number): StrixReport["grade"] {
+  if (score >= 90) return "A";
+  if (score >= 80) return "B";
+  if (score >= 70) return "C";
+  if (score >= 60) return "D";
+  return "F";
+}
+
+function parseCookie(raw: string): StrixCookie {
+  const [pair, ...attributes] = raw.split(";").map((part) => part.trim());
+  const name = pair.split("=")[0] || "(unnamed)";
+  const flags = new Set(attributes.map((attribute) => attribute.toLowerCase()));
+  const sameSite = attributes.find((attribute) => /^samesite=/i.test(attribute))?.split("=")[1] ?? "unset";
+  return { name, secure: flags.has("secure"), httpOnly: flags.has("httponly"), sameSite };
+}
+
+/** Runs the passive scan. Never throws: network failures become an unreachable report. */
+export async function runStrixScan(target: string): Promise<StrixReport> {
+  const normalised = /^https?:\/\//i.test(target.trim()) ? target.trim() : `https://${target.trim()}`;
+  const base: StrixReport = {
+    target: normalised,
+    scannedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    reachable: false,
+    httpStatus: null,
+    score: 0,
+    grade: "F",
+    findings: [],
+    headers: {},
+    cookies: [],
+    surface: [],
+    notes: [
+      "Passive scan only: one GET request, no redirects followed, no exploit payload sent.",
+      "Private, loopback and link-local targets are refused to prevent SSRF.",
+    ],
+  };
+  if (!isPassiveScanTarget(normalised)) {
+    return { ...base, error: "Target must be a public http(s) URL. Private, loopback and credential-bearing URLs are refused.", findings: [], score: 0, grade: "F" };
+  }
+
+  const controller = new AbortController();
+  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 12_000);
+  try {
+    const response = await fetch(normalised, {
+      method: "GET",
+      redirect: "manual",
+      signal: controller.signal,
+      headers: { "user-agent": "QASyn-Strix-Passive/1.0 (+defensive header audit)", accept: "text/html,application/xhtml+xml" },
+    });
+    clearTimeout(timer);
+
+    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
+    response.headers.forEach((value, key) => {
+      headers[key.toLowerCase()] = value;
+    });
+    const setCookies =
+      typeof response.headers.getSetCookie === "function"
+        ? response.headers.getSetCookie()
+        : headers["set-cookie"]
+          ? [headers["set-cookie"]]
+          : [];
+    const cookies = setCookies.map(parseCookie);
+    const contentType = headers["content-type"] ?? "";
+    const body = response.status < 400 && /text\/html|application\/xhtml/i.test(contentType) ? (await response.text()).slice(0, 200_000) : "";
+    const isHttps = normalised.startsWith("https:");
+
+    const findings: StrixFinding[] = [];
+    const add = (
+      id: string,
+      title: string,
+      severity: StrixSeverity,
+      category: StrixFinding["category"],
+      passed: boolean,
+      evidence: string,
+      remediation: string,
+      owasp: string,
+    ) => findings.push({ id, title, severity, category, passed, evidence, remediation, owasp });
+
+    const csp = headers["content-security-policy"];
+    add(
+      "csp-present",
+      "Content-Security-Policy",
+      "high",
+      "headers",
+      Boolean(csp),
+      csp ? csp.slice(0, 180) : "Header missing",
+      "Define a default-src 'self' policy and only allow the hosts actually used by scripts, styles and frames.",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+    if (csp) {
+      const weak = /unsafe-inline|unsafe-eval/i.test(csp) || !/default-src|script-src/i.test(csp);
+      add(
+        "csp-strict",
+        "CSP is restrictive",
+        "medium",
+        "headers",
+        !weak,
+        weak ? "Policy allows unsafe-inline/unsafe-eval or has no default-src/script-src." : "No unsafe-inline/unsafe-eval directive detected.",
+        "Remove unsafe-inline/unsafe-eval; use nonces or hashes for inline scripts and styles.",
+        "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+      );
+    }
+
+    const hsts = headers["strict-transport-security"];
+    if (isHttps) {
+      const maxAge = Number(/max-age=(\d+)/i.exec(hsts ?? "")?.[1] ?? 0);
+      add(
+        "hsts",
+        "Strict-Transport-Security",
+        "high",
+        "transport",
+        Boolean(hsts) && maxAge >= 15_552_000,
+        hsts ?? "Header missing",
+        "Serve Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains; preload on HTTPS responses.",
+        "A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures",
+      );
+    } else {
+      add("https", "HTTPS transport", "high", "transport", false, "Target is served over plain HTTP.", "Redirect all HTTP traffic to HTTPS and enable HSTS.", "A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures");
+    }
+
+    const frame = headers["x-frame-options"];
+    const frameAllowed = /deny|sameorigin/i.test(frame ?? "") || /frame-ancestors/i.test(csp ?? "");
+    add(
+      "frame",
+      "Clickjacking protection",
+      "medium",
+      "headers",
+      frameAllowed,
+      frame || (csp ? "Checked CSP frame-ancestors" : "No X-Frame-Options and no frame-ancestors"),
+      "Send X-Frame-Options: DENY (or SAMEORIGIN) and CSP frame-ancestors 'self'.",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+
+    add(
+      "nosniff",
+      "X-Content-Type-Options",
+      "low",
+      "headers",
+      /nosniff/i.test(headers["x-content-type-options"] ?? ""),
+      headers["x-content-type-options"] ?? "Header missing",
+      "Always send X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff.",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+
+    const referrer = headers["referrer-policy"];
+    add(
+      "referrer",
+      "Referrer-Policy",
+      "low",
+      "headers",
+      Boolean(referrer) && !/unsafe-url/i.test(referrer),
+      referrer ?? "Header missing",
+      "Use Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin (or no-referrer).",
+      "A01:2021 Broken Access Control",
+    );
+
+    add(
+      "permissions",
+      "Permissions-Policy",
+      "low",
+      "headers",
+      Boolean(headers["permissions-policy"]),
+      headers["permissions-policy"] ?? "Header missing",
+      "Disable unused browser features: camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=().",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+
+    const insecureCookies = cookies.filter((cookie) => !cookie.secure || !cookie.httpOnly || cookie.sameSite.toLowerCase() === "none" || cookie.sameSite === "unset");
+    add(
+      "cookies",
+      "Cookie flags",
+      cookies.length === 0 ? "info" : "high",
+      "cookies",
+      insecureCookies.length === 0,
+      cookies.length === 0 ? "No Set-Cookie header on this response." : `${cookies.length} cookie(s), ${insecureCookies.length} missing Secure/HttpOnly/SameSite.`,
+      "Set Secure, HttpOnly and SameSite=Lax (or Strict) on every session cookie.",
+      "A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures",
+    );
+
+    const server = headers.server;
+    add(
+      "server-banner",
+      "Server banner disclosure",
+      "low",
+      "exposure",
+      !server,
+      server ? `Server: ${server}` : "No Server header.",
+      "Remove or genericise the Server and X-Powered-By headers to reduce fingerprinting.",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+
+    const poweredBy = headers["x-powered-by"] ?? headers["x-aspnet-version"];
+    add(
+      "tech-banner",
+      "Framework version disclosure",
+      "low",
+      "exposure",
+      !poweredBy,
+      poweredBy ?? "No framework version header.",
+      "Remove X-Powered-By / X-AspNet-Version and verbose error pages in production.",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+
+    const cors = headers["access-control-allow-origin"];
+    add(
+      "cors",
+      "CORS wildcard",
+      "medium",
+      "exposure",
+      cors !== "*",
+      cors ? `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: ${cors}` : "No wildcard CORS header.",
+      "Restrict Access-Control-Allow-Origin to trusted origins; never combine '*' with credentials.",
+      "A01:2021 Broken Access Control",
+    );
+
+    const sourceMap = /sourceMappingURL=([^\s*]+)/i.exec(body)?.[1];
+    add(
+      "sourcemaps",
+      "Production source maps",
+      "medium",
+      "exposure",
+      !sourceMap,
+      sourceMap ? `sourceMappingURL=${sourceMap}` : "No sourceMappingURL in the returned HTML.",
+      "Do not publish .map files in production, or restrict them to authenticated internal users.",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+
+    const directoryListing = /<title>\s*Index of \//i.test(body);
+    add(
+      "directory-listing",
+      "Directory listing",
+      "high",
+      "exposure",
+      !directoryListing,
+      directoryListing ? "HTML title looks like an Apache/nginx directory index." : "No directory index detected.",
+      "Disable autoindex/directory browsing on the web server.",
+      "A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration",
+    );
+
+    const mixedForm = /<form[^>]+action=["']http:\/\//i.test(body);
+    add(
+      "mixed-form",
+      "Insecure form action",
+      "high",
+      "exposure",
+      !mixedForm,
+      mixedForm ? "A form posts to a plain HTTP action." : "No plain-HTTP form action detected.",
+      "Post forms to HTTPS endpoints only and enable HSTS.",
+      "A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures",
+    );
+
+    const surface = [
+      isHttps ? "HTTPS" : "HTTP (insecure)",
+      server ? `Server: ${server}` : "Server banner hidden",
+      poweredBy ? `Framework: ${poweredBy}` : "Framework version hidden",
+      `${cookies.length} cookie(s)`,
+      sourceMap ? "Source maps exposed" : "No source maps",
+      csp ? "CSP present" : "CSP absent",
+      hsts ? `HSTS ${hsts}` : "HSTS absent",
+    ];
+
+    const failed = findings.filter((finding) => !finding.passed && finding.severity !== "info");
+    const score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, 100 - failed.reduce((total, finding) => total + severityPenalty(finding.severity), 0)));
+
+    return {
+      ...base,
+      reachable: true,
+      httpStatus: response.status,
+      score,
+      grade: gradeFor(score),
+      findings,
+      headers,
+      cookies,
+      surface,
+      notes: [
+        ...base.notes,
+        `HTTP ${response.status}${response.status >= 300 && response.status < 400 ? ` · redirect to ${headers.location ?? "unknown"}` : ""}`,
+        `${findings.filter((finding) => finding.passed).length}/${findings.length} checks passed.`,
+      ],
+    };
+  } catch (error) {
+    clearTimeout(timer);
+    return {
+      ...base,
+      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Target did not answer.",
+      score: 0,
+      grade: "F",
+    };
+  }
+}

+/** Runs a real Windows PowerShell command and returns stdout+stderr (never throws). */
+function psRun(command: string): string {
+  try {
+    return execSync(`${command} 2>&1`, { encoding: "utf8", timeout: 120_000, windowsHide: true });
+  } catch (error) {
+    const failure = error as { stdout?: string | Buffer; stderr?: string | Buffer; message?: string };
+    return `${String(failure.stdout ?? "")}${String(failure.stderr ?? "")}${String(failure.message ?? "")}`;
+  }
+}

+            check("PART 7 Run History card shows automated trigger and author", cardText.includes("⚙") && cardText.includes("👤"), cardText.slice(0, 160));
+            check("PART 7 Run History card shows time and duration", cardText.includes("⏳"), cardText.slice(0, 160));
+            const modePill = cards.first().getByTestId("run-history-mode");
+            const modeText = ((await modePill.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").trim();
+            check("PART 7 Run History card shows Standalone Run or plan pill", (await modePill.count()) > 0 && modeText.length > 0, `${modeText} · ${cardText.slice(0, 120)}`);
+            if (/Failed/.test(cardText)) check("PART 7 failed Run History card shows Failed at Step X", /Failed at Step \d+/.test(cardText), cardText.slice(0, 160));
+            const autoFix = cards.first().getByText("Auto-fix rerun");
+            check("PART 7 Run History exposes Auto-fix rerun when applicable", (await autoFix.count()) > 0 || !/Auto-fix/.test(cardText), "auto-fix badge conditional");
+          }
+        }
+      }
+    } finally {
+      if (createdStudioCase) await db.testCase.delete({ where: { id: studioCaseId } }).catch(() => undefined);
+    }
+
+    // 7.6 — MCP page: OpenCode first, real cqa_ token, 5 prompts, skills, Context7, live tool.
+    await goto(page, "/dashboard/mcp");
+    await page.locator('[data-client="opencode"]').first().waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 15_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    const firstClient = await page.locator("[data-client]").first().getAttribute("data-client");
+    check("PART 7 MCP lists OpenCode first", firstClient === "opencode", firstClient ?? "none");
+    check("PART 7 MCP labels OpenCode with free models", (await page.locator('[data-client="opencode"]').first().innerText()).includes("OpenCode (Free AI Models)"));
+    const mcpBody = await body(page);
+    for (const model of ["opencode/big-pickle", "opencode/glm-4.7-free", "opencode/grok-code"]) {
+      check(`PART 7 MCP lists free model ${model}`, mcpBody.includes(model));
+    }
+    check("PART 7 MCP shows the OpenCode command", mcpBody.includes("opencode --model opencode/big-pickle") && mcpBody.includes("/models"));
+    check("PART 7 MCP never shows cqa_REPLACE_WITH_YOUR_TOKEN", !mcpBody.includes("cqa_REPLACE_WITH_YOUR_TOKEN"));
+    const tokenValue = page.locator('[data-testid="mcp-token-value"]').first();
+    await tokenValue.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 10_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    const tokenText = ((await tokenValue.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").trim();
+    check("PART 7 MCP embeds a real auto-provisioned cqa_ token", /^cqa_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}/.test(tokenText), tokenText ? `${tokenText.slice(0, 12)}… (${tokenText.length} chars)` : "missing");
+    const promptCount = await page.locator("[data-prompt]").count();
+    check("PART 7 MCP exposes 5 copyable OpenCode prompts", promptCount === 5, `${promptCount} prompt(s)`);
+    check(
+      "PART 7 OpenCode prompts expose Copy Test Prompt for OpenCode",
+      (await page.locator('[data-prompt] button[aria-label="Copy Test Prompt for OpenCode"]').count()) === promptCount && promptCount > 0,
+      `${await page.locator('[data-prompt] button[aria-label="Copy Test Prompt for OpenCode"]').count()} button(s)`,
+    );
+    if (promptCount > 0) {
+      const firstPrompt = ((await page.locator("[data-prompt]").first().innerText()) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+      check("PART 7 OpenCode prompt targets a qasyn_ tool", firstPrompt.includes("qasyn_"), firstPrompt.slice(0, 140));
+    }
+    let skillCount = 0;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
+      skillCount = await page.locator("[data-skill]").count();
+      if (skillCount >= 6) break;
+      await page.waitForTimeout(400);
+    }
+    check("PART 7 MCP lists the 6 Agent Skills", skillCount === 6, `${skillCount} skill(s)`);
+    check("PART 7 MCP exposes 3 Agent Skills exports", (await page.locator("[data-export]").count()) === 3, `${await page.locator("[data-export]").count()} export(s)`);
+    const context7Section = page.locator('[data-testid="context7-resolver"]').first();
+    check("PART 7 MCP embeds the Context7 resolver", (await context7Section.count()) > 0);
+    if ((await context7Section.count()) > 0) {
+      await context7Section.getByText(/local deterministic/).first().waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 15_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+      const c7Text = ((await context7Section.innerText()) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+      check("PART 7 Context7 resolver returns versioned rules", /Playwright/.test(c7Text) && /score \d+/.test(c7Text), c7Text.slice(0, 180));
+    }
+
+    const context7Api = await apiJson(page, "/api/dashboard/context7", "POST", { query: "Playwright locator for checkout" });
+    const c7body = context7Api.body as { rules?: string[]; library?: { name?: string; version?: string }; injectedPrompt?: string; resolved?: boolean };
+    check(
+      "PART 7 Context7 API resolves Playwright docs",
+      context7Api.status === 200 && (c7body.library?.name ?? "").toLowerCase().includes("playwright") && (c7body.rules?.length ?? 0) > 0 && Boolean(c7body.injectedPrompt),
+      `${c7body.library?.name ?? "?"} v${c7body.library?.version ?? "?"} · ${c7body.rules?.length ?? 0} rules`,
+    );
+
+    const mcpRun = page.locator('[data-testid="mcp-tool-run"]').first();
+    if ((await mcpRun.count()) > 0) {
+      await mcpRun.click();
+      const mcpResponse = page.locator('[data-testid="mcp-tool-response"]').first();
+      await mcpResponse.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 30_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+      const responseText = ((await mcpResponse.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
+      check("PART 7 MCP tool runner returns a live JSON-RPC 200", responseText.includes('"status": 200') && responseText.includes('"result"'), responseText.slice(0, 180));
+    }
+
+    const exportRes = await apiJson(page, "/api/dashboard/agent-skills", "POST", { action: "export", target: "claude" });
+    const exportBody = exportRes.body as { path?: string; content?: string };
+    check(
+      "PART 7 Agent Skills export writes .claude/CLAUDE.md",
+      exportRes.status === 200 && exportBody.path === ".claude/CLAUDE.md" && /Playwright Self-Healing/.test(exportBody.content ?? ""),
+      `${exportBody.path ?? "missing"} · ${(exportBody.content ?? "").length} chars`,
+    );
+
+    // 7.7 — Graphify Map: API + tab on crawl details + interactive SVG on code analysis.
+    const graphApi = await apiJson(page, "/api/dashboard/graphify", "GET");
+    const graphData = graphApi.body as unknown as {
+      nodes?: unknown[];
+      edges?: unknown[];
+      stats?: Record<string, number>;
+    };
+    check(
+      "PART 7 Graphify API returns nodes and edges",
+      graphApi.status === 200 && Array.isArray(graphData.nodes) && Array.isArray(graphData.edges),
+      `${graphData.nodes?.length ?? 0} node(s) · ${graphData.edges?.length ?? 0} edge(s)`,
+    );
+    check(
+      "PART 7 Graphify API covers URL/DOM/Requirement/Test/PR",
+      ["url", "dom", "requirement", "test", "pr"].every((kind) => typeof graphData.stats?.[kind] === "number"),
+      JSON.stringify(graphData.stats ?? {}),
+    );
+
+    const crawlForGraph = await db.crawlSession.findFirst({
+      where: { organisationId: cgiId },
+      orderBy: { startedAt: "desc" },
+      select: { id: true },
+    });
+    if (crawlForGraph) {
+      await goto(page, `/dashboard/crawl/${crawlForGraph.id}`);
+      const graphTab = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Graphify Map", exact: true }).first();
+      await graphTab.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 12_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+      check("PART 7 crawl details expose the Graphify Map tab", (await graphTab.count()) > 0);
+      if ((await graphTab.count()) > 0) {
+        await graphTab.click();
+        const graphMap = page.locator('[data-testid="graphify-map"]');
+        await graphMap.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 12_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+        check("PART 7 crawl Graphify Map renders", (await graphMap.count()) > 0);
+        check("PART 7 Graphify Map has 5 kind filters", (await graphMap.locator("[data-filter]").count()) === 5, `${await graphMap.locator("[data-filter]").count()} filter(s)`);
+        check("PART 7 Graphify Map has zoom controls", (await graphMap.locator('[data-testid="graphify-zoom-in"]').count()) > 0 && (await graphMap.locator('[data-testid="graphify-zoom-out"]').count()) > 0);
+        const beforeZoom = ((await graphMap.innerText()) || "").includes("100%");
+        await graphMap.locator('[data-testid="graphify-zoom-in"]').click().catch(() => undefined);
+        await page.waitForTimeout(300);
+        const afterZoom = ((await graphMap.innerText()) || "").includes("115%");
+        check("PART 7 Graphify zoom changes from 100% to 115%", beforeZoom && afterZoom, `before=${beforeZoom} after=${afterZoom}`);
+        const prFilter = graphMap.locator('[data-filter="pr"]');
+        const beforePressed = await prFilter.getAttribute("aria-pressed");
+        await prFilter.click().catch(() => undefined);
+        await page.waitForTimeout(200);
+        const afterPressed = await prFilter.getAttribute("aria-pressed");
+        check("PART 7 Graphify filters toggle", beforePressed !== afterPressed, `${beforePressed} → ${afterPressed}`);
+        if ((graphData.nodes?.length ?? 0) > 0) {
+          check("PART 7 Graphify Map renders interactive SVG nodes", (await graphMap.locator("svg [data-node-kind]").count()) > 0, `${await graphMap.locator("svg [data-node-kind]").count()} node(s)`);
+        }
+      }
+    } else {
+      check("PART 7 crawl details expose the Graphify Map tab", false, "no crawl session in the organisation");
+    }
+
+    await goto(page, "/dashboard/code-analysis");
+    const codeGraph = page.locator('[data-testid="graphify-map"]').first();
+    await codeGraph.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 12_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    check("PART 7 code analysis embeds the Graphify Map", (await codeGraph.count()) > 0);
+    if ((await codeGraph.count()) > 0 && (graphData.nodes?.length ?? 0) > 0) {
+      check("PART 7 code analysis Graphify Map renders SVG nodes", (await codeGraph.locator("svg [data-node-kind]").count()) > 0);
+    }
+
+    // 7.8 — Magic Locator API covers every accessibility strategy.
+    const magicClick = await apiJson(page, "/api/dashboard/magic-locator", "POST", { action: "Click", target: "Pay now", value: "", selector: "" });
+    const magicInput = await apiJson(page, "/api/dashboard/magic-locator", "POST", { action: "Enter Text", target: "Work e-mail", value: "qa@cgi.com", selector: "" });
+    const magicStrategies = new Set(
+      [
+        ...(((magicClick.body as { candidates?: Array<{ strategy?: string }> }).candidates ?? [])),
+        ...(((magicInput.body as { candidates?: Array<{ strategy?: string }> }).candidates ?? [])),
+      ].map((candidate) => String(candidate.strategy)),
+    );
+    const magicBest = (magicClick.body as { best?: { selector?: string; score?: number } }).best;
+    check(
+      "PART 7 Magic Locator API scores Role/Test ID/Label/CSS",
+      magicClick.status === 200 && magicInput.status === 200 && ["Role", "Test ID", "Label", "CSS"].every((strategy) => magicStrategies.has(strategy)),
+      [...magicStrategies].join(", "),
+    );
+    check("PART 7 Magic Locator returns a Best Locator", Boolean(magicBest?.selector) && Number(magicBest?.score ?? 0) > 0, `${magicBest?.selector ?? "missing"} · ${magicBest?.score ?? 0}`);
+
+    // 7.9 — Strix passive scanner: SSRF refusal, graceful public scan, both pages.
+    const strixBlocked = await apiJson(page, "/api/dashboard/strix", "POST", { url: "http://localhost:3000" });
+    check("PART 7 Strix refuses localhost (SSRF-safe)", strixBlocked.status === 400, `status ${strixBlocked.status}`);
+    const strixPublic = await apiJson(page, "/api/dashboard/strix", "POST", { url: "https://example.com" });
+    const strixReport = strixPublic.body as { findings?: unknown[]; notes?: unknown[]; score?: number; grade?: string; reachable?: boolean; error?: string };
+    check(
+      "PART 7 Strix scans a public target gracefully",
+      strixPublic.status === 200 && Array.isArray(strixReport.findings) && Array.isArray(strixReport.notes) && typeof strixReport.score === "number" && typeof strixReport.grade === "string",
+      strixReport.reachable ? `score ${strixReport.score} · grade ${strixReport.grade}` : `unreachable handled · ${strixReport.error ?? "no error"}`,
+    );
+
+    await goto(page, "/dashboard/security-testing");
+    const strixUi = page.locator('[data-testid="strix-scanner"]').first();
+    await strixUi.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 12_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    check("PART 7 Security testing embeds Strix", (await strixUi.count()) > 0 && (await strixUi.innerText()).includes("Strix AI Security & OWASP Inspector"));
+    if ((await strixUi.count()) > 0) {
+      await strixUi.getByLabel("Strix scan target").fill("http://localhost:3000").catch(() => undefined);
+      await strixUi.locator('[data-testid="strix-scan"]').click().catch(() => undefined);
+      const strixAlert = strixUi.getByRole("alert").first();
+      await strixAlert.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 15_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+      check("PART 7 Strix UI reports the blocked target", (await strixAlert.count()) > 0 && /public http\(s\)/i.test(await strixAlert.innerText().catch(() => "")), ((await strixAlert.innerText().catch(() => "")) || "").slice(0, 140));
+    }
+
+    await goto(page, "/dashboard/red-team");
+    const strixRed = page.locator('[data-testid="strix-scanner"]').first();
+    await strixRed.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 12_000 }).catch(() => undefined);
+    check("PART 7 Red team embeds Strix", (await strixRed.count()) > 0 && (await strixRed.innerText()).includes("Strix AI Security & OWASP Inspector"));
+
+    check("PART 7 has no page errors", moduleErrors.length === 0, moduleErrors.slice(0, 2).join(" | "));
+    await shot(page, "part7-integrations");
+  } finally {
+    await db.tunnelMachine.delete({ where: { id: part7Machine.id } }).catch(() => undefined);
+  }
+
   await db.$disconnect();
 
   // ── Global integrity ────────────────────────────────────────────
